




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

abhidhammapiṭakai

paṭṭhānapāḷi

(paṭhamau bhāgau)

dhammānulaumai

tikapaṭṭhānaṃ

(1) pac‍cayuddaisau

haitupac‍cayau, ārammaṇapac‍cayau, adhipatipac‍cayau, anantarapac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayau, sahajātapac‍cayau, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayau, puraijātapac‍cayau, pacchājātapac‍cayau, āsaivanapac‍cayau, kammapac‍cayau, vipākapac‍cayau, āhārapac‍cayau, indriyapac‍cayau, jhānapac‍cayau, maggapac‍cayau, sampayuttapac‍cayau, vippayuttapac‍cayau, atthipac‍cayau, natthipac‍cayau, vigatapac‍cayau, avigatapac‍cayauti|

(2) pac‍cayaniddaisau

1. haitupac‍cayauti  – haitū haitusampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau pac‍cayauti (syā॰)|

2. ārammaṇapac‍cayauti – rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā  taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gandhāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rasāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sabbai dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

yaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ ārabbha yai yai dhammā uppaj‍janti cittacaitasikā dhammā, tai tai dhammā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

3. adhipatipac‍cayauti – chandādhipati chandasampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vīriyādhipati vīriyasampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cittādhipati cittasampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vīmaṃsādhipati vīmaṃsasampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

yaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ garuṃ katvā yai yai dhammā uppaj‍janti cittacaitasikā dhammā, tai tai dhammā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

4. anantarapac‍cayauti  – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā kusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā kusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ  abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

yaisaṃ  yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarā yai yai dhammā uppaj‍janti cittacaitasikā dhammā, tai tai dhammā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

5. samanantarapac‍cayauti – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

ghāṇaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manaudhātu taṃsampayuttakā ca dhammā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā kusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā kusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

yaisaṃ  yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarā yai yai dhammā uppaj‍janti cittacaitasikā dhammā, tai tai dhammā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

6. sahajātapac‍cayauti  – cattārau khandhā arūpinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cattārau mahābhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| auk‍kantikkhaṇai nāmarūpaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cittacaitasikā dhammā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpinau dhammā arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ kiñ‍ci kālai kañ‍ci kālaṃ (syā॰) sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, kiñ‍ci kālai na sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

7. añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayauti – cattārau khandhā arūpinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cattārau mahābhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| auk‍kantikkhaṇai nāmarūpaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

8. nissayapac‍cayauti – cattārau khandhā arūpinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cattārau mahābhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| auk‍kantikkhaṇai nāmarūpaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cittacaitasikā dhammā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā upādārūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ  nissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ghānāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| jivhāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manaudhātu ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu ca vattanti, taṃ rūpaṃ manaudhātuyā ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā ca taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

9. upanissayapac‍cayauti – purimā purimā kusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā kusalā  dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ kaisañ‍ci upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā kusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ kaisañ‍ci upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ abyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā abyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

utubhaujanampi upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puggalaupi upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saināsanampi upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

10. puraijātapac‍cayauti – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ghānāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| jivhāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gandhāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rasāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ  saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manaudhātu ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  ca vattanti, taṃ rūpaṃ manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ kiñ‍ci kālai puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, kiñ‍ci kālai na puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

11. pacchājātapac‍cayauti – pacchājātā cittacaitasikā dhammā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

12. āsaivanapac‍cayauti – purimā purimā kusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā akusalā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| purimā purimā kiriyābyākatā dhammā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ dhammānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

13. kammapac‍cayauti – kusalākusalaṃ kammaṃ vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

14. vipākapac‍cayauti  – vipākā cattārau khandhā arūpinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

15. āhārapac‍cayauti – kabaḷīkārau kabaḷiṅkārau (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰) āhārau imassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpinau āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

16. indriyapac‍cayauti  – cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautindriyaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ghānindriyaṃ  ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| jivhindriyaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyindriyaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

arūpinau indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

17. jhānapac‍cayauti – jhānaṅgāni jhānasampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

18. maggapac‍cayauti – maggaṅgāni maggasampayuttakānaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃsamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

19. sampayuttapac‍cayauti – cattārau khandhā arūpinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

20. vippayuttapac‍cayauti – rūpinau dhammā arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arūpinau dhammā rūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

21. atthipac‍cayauti  – cattārau khandhā arūpinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cattārau mahābhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| auk‍kantikkhaṇai nāmarūpaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cittacaitasikā dhammā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ghānāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| jivhāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

rūpāyatanaṃ  cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gandhāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rasāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manaudhātu ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  ca vattanti, taṃ rūpaṃ manaudhātuyā ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā ca taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

22. natthipac‍cayauti – samanantaraniruddhā cittacaitasikā dhammā paṭuppan‍nānaṃ cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

23. vigatapac‍cayauti – samanantaravigatā cittacaitasikā dhammā paṭuppan‍nānaṃ cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

24. avigatapac‍cayauti  – cattārau khandhā arūpinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cattārau mahābhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| auk‍kantikkhaṇai nāmarūpaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cittacaitasikā dhammā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā upādārūpānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ghānāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| jivhāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

rūpāyatanaṃ  cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| gandhāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rasāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ manaudhātuyā taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manaudhātu ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu ca vattanti, taṃ rūpaṃ manaudhātuyā ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā ca taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pac‍cayaniddaisau|

3. pucchāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

aikamūlakaṃ

(1.) kusalapadaṃ

25. siyā  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā|

(2) akusalapadaṃ

26. siyā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā|

(3) abyākatapadaṃ

27. siyā abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā|

(4) kusalābyākatapadaṃ

28. siyā kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā|

(5) akusalābyākatapadaṃ

29. siyā  akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā|

(6) kusalākusalapadaṃ

30. siyā  kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca  akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā|

(7) kusalākusalābyākatapadaṃ

31. siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya  haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā| siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā|

haitupac‍cayavārau|

32. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya ārammaṇapac‍cayā|

(yathā haitupac‍cayau vitthāritau, aivaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayaupi vitthāraitabbau vācanāmaggaina|)

33. siyā  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya adhipatipac‍cayā… anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā… pacchājātapac‍cayā… āsaivanapac‍cayā… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā… āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā|

34. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya avigatapac‍cayā…pai॰… akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya… akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya… abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya… kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ… akusalau  ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ… kusalau ca akusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ… kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ avigatapac‍cayā|

(yathā haitupac‍cayau vitthāritau, aivaṃ avigatapac‍cayaupi vitthāraitabbau vācanāmaggaina|)

aikamūlakaṃ|

dumūlakādi

haitumūlakaṃ

35. siyā  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… siyā kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā|

36. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā  …pai॰… haitupac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā… haitupac‍cayā samanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā|

dumūlakaṃ|

37. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā|

timūlakaṃ|

38. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā|

catumūlakaṃ|

(pañ‍camūlakādikā saṃkhittā| aikamūlakaṃ, dumūlakaṃ, timūlakaṃ, catumūlakaṃ, pañ‍camūlakaṃ, sabbamūlakaṃ asammuyhantaina vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitumūlakaṃ|

ārammaṇamūlakādi

39. siyā  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya ārammaṇapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā… ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… ārammaṇapac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā|

siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya adhipatipac‍cayā… anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā  haitupac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā|

dumūlakaṃ|

40. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā|

41. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… vigatapac‍cayā|

(aikaikassa padassa aikamūlakaṃ, dumūlakaṃ, timūlakaṃ, catumūlakaṃ, pañ‍camūlakaṃ, sabbamūlakaṃ asammuyhantaina vitthāraitabbaṃ| )

(ka) tikañ‍ca paṭṭhānavaraṃ dukuttamaṃ,

dukaṃ tikañ‍caiva tikaṃ dukañ‍ca|

tikaṃ tikañ‍caiva dukaṃ dukañ‍ca,

cha anulaumamhi nayā sugambhīrāti||

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

42. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya nahaitupac‍cayā|

(yathā  anulaumai haitupac‍cayau vitthāritau, aivaṃ pac‍canīyaipi nahaitupac‍cayau vitthāraitabbau|)

43. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya naārammaṇapac‍cayā… naadhipatipac‍cayā… naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… nasahajātapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nanissayapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā… napacchājātapac‍cayā  … naāsaivanapac‍cayā… nakammapac‍cayā… navipākapac‍cayā… naāhārapac‍cayā… naindriyapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā… namaggapac‍cayā… nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā… nauatthipac‍cayā… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā… nauavigatapac‍cayā|

44. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…|

(yathā anulaumai aikaikassa padassa aikamūlakaṃ, dumūlakaṃ, timūlakaṃ, catumūlakaṃ, yāva taivīsatimūlakaṃ aivaṃ pac‍canīyaipi vitthāraitabbaṃ| )

(kha) tikañ‍ca paṭṭhānavaraṃ dukuttamaṃ,

dukaṃ tikañ‍caiva tikaṃ dukañ‍ca|

tikaṃ tikañ‍caiva dukaṃ dukañ‍ca,

cha pac‍canīyamhi nayā sugambhīrāti||

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

45. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā… siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā|

(yathā anulaumai haitupac‍cayau vitthāritau, aivaṃ anulaumapac‍canīyaipi padaṃ  vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

46. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā… haitupac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā nauavigatapac‍cayā|

47. siyā  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā… haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā nauavigatapac‍cayā|

haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā nauavigatapac‍cayā|

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā nauavigatapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā samanantarapac‍cayā sahajātapac‍cayā añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā nissayapac‍cayā upanissayapac‍cayā puraijātapac‍cayā pacchājātapac‍cayā āsaivanapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā vipākapac‍cayā āhārapac‍cayā indriyapac‍cayā jhānapac‍cayā maggapac‍cayā sampayuttapac‍cayā vippayuttapac‍cayā atthipac‍cayā natthipac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā nauavigatapac‍cayā|

48. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya ārammaṇapac‍cayā… adhipatipac‍cayā… anantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā … avigatapac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā nauvigatapac‍cayā|

avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā nauvigatapac‍cayā|

avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā nauvigatapac‍cayā|

avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā samanantarapac‍cayā sahajātapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

(ga) tikañ‍ca paṭṭhānavaraṃ dukuttamaṃ,

dukaṃ tikañ‍caiva tikaṃ dukañ‍ca|

tikaṃ tikañ‍caiva dukaṃ dukañ‍ca,

cha anulaumapac‍canīyamhi nayā sugambhīrāti||

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

49. siyā  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā| siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya nahaitupac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… nahaitupac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā|

50. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā|

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayā|

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauatthipac‍cayā naunatthipac‍cayā nauvigatapac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā|

51. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya naārammaṇapac‍cayā  haitupac‍cayā|

52. siyā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jaiyya naārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… naārammaṇapac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauavigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā… nauavigatapac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauavigatapac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā|

nauavigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauavigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā|

nauavigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… nauatthipac‍cayā naunatthipac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā|

(gha) tikañ‍ca  paṭṭhānavaraṃ dukuttamaṃ,

dukaṃ tikañ‍caiva tikaṃ dukañ‍ca|

tikaṃ tikañ‍caiva dukaṃ dukañ‍ca,

cha pac‍canīyānulaumamhi nayā sugambhīrāti||

pucchāvārau|

niddaisavārai taivīsatipac‍cayā|

1. kusalattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

anulaumaṃ – haitupac‍cayau

53. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau  khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

54. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

55. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau  uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai  mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anantara-samanantarapac‍cayā

56. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā| (anantarampi samanantarampi ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

sahajātapac‍cayau

57. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti sahajātapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca  dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  sahajātapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ; āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai  paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ; utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ; asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

58. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau  khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vatthu ca, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau vatthu ca, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vatthu ca; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā| (1)

nissayapac‍cayau

59. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nissayapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…| (nissayapac‍cayaṃ sahajātapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

upanissayapac‍cayau

60. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati upanissayapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…| (upanissayapac‍cayaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

puraijātapac‍cayau

61. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai  paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

62. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

63. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; asañ‍ñasattānaṃ  aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

64. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā; tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

āhārapac‍cayau

65. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ  paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ|

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

indriyapac‍cayau

66. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati indriyapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (indriyapac‍cayaṃ kammapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

jhāna-maggapac‍cayā

67. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā| (jhānapac‍cayampi maggapac‍cayampi haitupac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

sampayuttapac‍cayau

68. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati sampayuttapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…| (sampayuttapac‍cayaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

vippayuttapac‍cayau

69. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca  aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau  dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau  uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| vatthu khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

atthipac‍cayau

70. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati atthipac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā|

(saṅkhitaṃ| atthipac‍cayaṃ sahajātapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

natthi-vigatapac‍cayā

71. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā| (natthipac‍cayampi vigatapac‍cayampi ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

avigatapac‍cayau

72. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati avigatapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā| (avigatapac‍cayaṃ sahajātapac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

(imai taivīsatipac‍cayā sajjhāyantaina vitthāraitabbā|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

73. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava|

gaṇanā haitumūlakā

dukaṃ

74. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai  tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai  tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava|

tikaṃ

75. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatiyā tīṇi anantarai tīṇi samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

dvādasakaṃ

76. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā samanantarapac‍cayā sahajātapac‍cayā añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā nissayapac‍cayā upanissayapac‍cayā puraijātapac‍cayā āsaivanapac‍cayā kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

bāvīsakaṃ

77. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… āsaivanapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā āhārapac‍cayā indriyapac‍cayā jhānapac‍cayā maggapac‍cayā sampayuttapac‍cayā vippayuttapac‍cayā atthipac‍cayā natthipac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

tairasakaṃ

78. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… puraijātapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā vipākapac‍cayā  āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, jhānai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

bāvīsakaṃ

79. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… puraijātapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā vipākapac‍cayā āhārapac‍cayā indriyapac‍cayā jhānapac‍cayā maggapac‍cayā sampayuttapac‍cayā vippayuttapac‍cayā atthipac‍cayā natthipac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā avigatai aikaṃ|

gaṇanā haitumūlakā|

ārammaṇādidukāni

(ārammaṇai ṭhitaina sabbattha tīṇaiva pañhā|)

80. ārammaṇapac‍cayā  haituyā tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

adhipatipac‍cayā haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai nava…pai॰…|

anantarapac‍cayā samanantarapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

sahajātapac‍cayā haituyā nava…pai॰…|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

nissayapac‍cayā haituyā nava…pai॰…|

upanissayapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

puraijātapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

āsaivanadukaṃ

81. āsaivanapac‍cayā  haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (āsaivanamūlakai vipākaṃ natthi|)

kammadukaṃ

82. kammapac‍cayā  haituyā nava…pai॰…|

vipākadukaṃ

83. vipākapac‍cayā haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ, adhipatiyā aikaṃ, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai aikaṃ, puraijātai aikaṃ, kammai aikaṃ, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, jhānai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ| (vipākamūlakai āsaivanaṃ natthi|)

āhārādidukāni

84. āhārapac‍cayā haituyā nava…pai॰…|

indriyapac‍cayā haituyā nava…pai॰…|

jhānapac‍cayā haituyā nava…pai॰…|

maggapac‍cayā haituyā nava…pai॰…|

sampayuttapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

vippayuttapac‍cayā  haituyā nava…pai॰…|

atthipac‍cayā haituyā nava…pai॰…|

natthipac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

vigatapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

avigatadukaṃ

85. avigatapac‍cayā haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi; adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi|

(aikaikaṃ pac‍cayaṃ mūlakaṃ kātūna sajjhāyantaina gaṇaitabbāti|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

pac‍canīyaṃ – nahaitupac‍cayau

86. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

87. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca  vatthu; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

88. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai  khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai  paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ … utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ  paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naanantara-nasamanantarapac‍cayā

89. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (naanantarapac‍cayampi nasamanantarapac‍cayampi naārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

90. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhirai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca  upādārūpaṃ; āhārasamuṭṭhānai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ; utusamuṭṭhānai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ; asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati na añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naupanissayapac‍cayau

91. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naupanissayapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (naupanissayapac‍cayaṃ naārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

napuraijātapac‍cayau

92. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ| (2)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

napacchājāta-naāsaivanapac‍cayā

93. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā  – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (napacchājātapac‍cayampi naāsaivanapac‍cayampi naadhipatipac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

nakammapac‍cayau

94. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca kusalā caitanā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca akusalā caitanā| (1)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kiriyābyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca kiriyābyākatā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

95. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

naāhārapac‍cayau

96. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ … utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naindriyapac‍cayau

97. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ; asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ| (1)

najhānapac‍cayau

98. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā; tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai  khandhā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

namaggapac‍cayau

99. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā; mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

nasampayuttapac‍cayau

100. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nasampayuttapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (naārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

navippayuttapac‍cayau

101. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā  – arūpai kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naunatthi-nauvigatapac‍cayā

102. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (naārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

103. nahaituyā  dvai, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

nahaitudukaṃ

104. nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā dvai, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

tikaṃ

105. nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, na anantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai aikaṃ, napacchājātai aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai aikaṃ, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

vīsakaṃ

106. nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā naupanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā  naāsaivanapac‍cayā nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā naāhārapac‍cayā naindriyapac‍cayā najhānapac‍cayā namaggapac‍cayā nasampayuttapac‍cayā navippayuttapac‍cayā naunatthipac‍cayā nauvigatai aikaṃ|

nahaitumūlakaṃ|

naārammaṇadukaṃ

107. naārammaṇapac‍cayā  nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai pañ‍ca, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca…pai॰…|

catuk‍kaṃ

108. naārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarai aikaṃ…pai॰… naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

naadhipatidukaṃ

109. naadhipatipac‍cayā nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

taivīsakaṃ (savipākaṃ)

138. haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… puraijātapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā vipākapac‍cayā āhārapac‍cayā indriyapac‍cayā jhānapac‍cayā maggapac‍cayā sampayuttapac‍cayā vippayuttapac‍cayā atthipac‍cayā natthipac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā avigatapac‍cayā napacchājātai aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ|

haitumūlakaṃ|

ārammaṇadukaṃ

ārammaṇamūlakaṃ|

189. avigatapac‍cayā haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

(yathā haitumūlakaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ
