aṭṭhakaṃ

230. nauvigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā sahajātai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, kammai aikaṃ, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, jhānai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

tairasakaṃ

231. nauvigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā sahajātai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, āhārai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

pan‍narasakaṃ

232. nauvigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā naāhārapac‍cayā sahajātai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

aikavīsakaṃ

233. nauvigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā naāhārapac‍cayā naindriyapac‍cayā najhānapac‍cayā namaggapac‍cayā nasampayuttapac‍cayā navippayuttapac‍cayā naunatthipac‍cayā sahajātai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

234. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ sahajātau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai sahajātaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau kusalau  ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

235. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai sahajātaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

236. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā  kaṭattā  ca rūpaṃ; khandhai sahajātaṃ vatthu, vatthuṃ sahajātā  khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ sahajātā tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai sahajātaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai sahajātā dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai sahajātaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

237. kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ sahajātau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca sahajātaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ sahajātau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca sahajātaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

(yathā paṭic‍cavārai aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

238. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava|

anulaumaṃ

(yathā paṭic‍cavāragaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

239. akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ sahajātau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai sahajātau vicikicchāsahagatau  uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ sahajātau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, khandhai sahajātaṃ vatthu, vatthuṃ sahajātā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ sahajātā tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai sahajātaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai sahajātā dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai sahajātaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ sahajātā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai sahajātaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

(yathā paṭic‍cavārai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

240. nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai  pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai  aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

241. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

242. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai dvai, anantarai dvai, samanantarai dvai, sahajātai dvai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai dvai, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai dvai, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai dvai, indriyai dvai, jhānai dvai, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai dvai, atthiyā dvai, natthiyā dvai, vigatai dvai, avigatai dvai|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

sahajātavārau|

(paṭic‍cattaṃ nāma sahajātattaṃ, sahajātattaṃ nāma paṭic‍cattaṃ|)

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

243. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

244. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

245. abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai pac‍cayā aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai pac‍cayā dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā| (2)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

246. kusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

247. akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā  aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

248. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

249. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

250. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā| (2)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ  pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā| (3)

251. kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

252. akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

253. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā|

(yathā haitupac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ  vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

anantara-samanantarapac‍cayā

254. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā|

(yathā ārammaṇapac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

sahajātapac‍cayau

255. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā|

(yathā haitupac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

256. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau  khandhā vatthu ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā vatthu ca, khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai pac‍cayā dvai mahābhūtā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai pac‍cayā dvai mahābhūtā, cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā|

(yathā ārammaṇapac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nissayapac‍cayau

257. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nissayapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā|

(yathā sahajātapac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

upanissayapac‍cayau

258. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati upanissayapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā| (ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

puraijātapac‍cayau

259. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ  pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai  pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā, cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (3)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

260. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā  – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā|

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…|

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰…|

kammapac‍cayau

261. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ  pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā…pai॰…| (5)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau…pai॰… abyākatau dhammau…pai॰… kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti kammapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau…pai॰… abyākatau dhammau…pai॰… akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti kammapac‍cayā, akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā…pai॰… akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

vipākapac‍cayau

262. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā| vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā khandhā|

āhārapac‍cayau

263. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā  khandhā| (paripuṇṇaṃ|)

indriyapac‍cayau

264. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati indriyapac‍cayā…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

(indriyapac‍cayā yathā kammapac‍cayā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

jhāna-maggapac‍cayā

265. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati jhānapac‍cayā…pai॰… maggapac‍cayā|

(jhānapac‍cayāpi maggapac‍cayāpi yathā haitupac‍cayā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

sampayuttapac‍cayau

266. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati sampayuttapac‍cayā| (ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

vippayuttapac‍cayau

267. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā, kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā, paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, kaṭattārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā, khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, vatthu khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ  pac‍cayā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā; cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (2)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (4)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā akusalā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (5)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā, kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vippayuttapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhā, akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca  pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, khandhā vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā, cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ khandhai vippayuttapac‍cayā| (3)

atthipac‍cayādi

268. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati atthipac‍cayā…pai॰… (atthipac‍cayā sahajātapac‍cayasadisaṃ kātabbaṃ| natthipac‍cayā vigatapac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ, avigatapac‍cayā sahajātapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

269. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai satta, kammai sattarasa, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai sattarasa, jhānai sattarasa, maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai sattarasa|

haitudukaṃ

270. haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai sattarasa…pai॰… avigatai sattarasa|

tikaṃ

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatiyā satta, (sabbattha satta) vipākai aikaṃ, avigatai satta…pai॰…|

dvādasakaṃ (sāsaivanaṃ)

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā samanantarapac‍cayā sahajātapac‍cayā añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā nissayapac‍cayā upanissayapac‍cayā puraijātapac‍cayā āsaivanapac‍cayā kammai satta, āhārai satta…pai॰… avigatai satta…pai॰…|

bāvīsakaṃ (sāsaivanaṃ)

haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… puraijātapac‍cayā āsaivanapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā āhārapac‍cayā…pai॰… vigatapac‍cayā avigatai satta|

tairasakaṃ (savipākaṃ)

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… puraijātapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā vipākapac‍cayā āhārai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ|

bāvīsakaṃ (savipākaṃ)

haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… puraijātapac‍cayā kammapac‍cayā vipākapac‍cayā āhārapac‍cayā…pai॰… vigatapac‍cayā avigatai aikaṃ| haitumūlakaṃ|

ārammaṇadukaṃ

271. ārammaṇapac‍cayā haituyā satta, adhipatiyā satta…pai॰… (ārammaṇamūlakaṃ yathā haitumūlakaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

adhipatidukaṃ

272. adhipatipac‍cayā haituyā sattarasa…pai॰…|

anantara-samanantaradukāni

273. anantarapac‍cayā samanantarapac‍cayā haituyā satta…pai॰…|

sahajātādidukāni

274. sahajātapac‍cayā…pai॰… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

āsaivanapac‍cayā haituyā satta, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai satta, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, kammai satta, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai satta, atthiyā satta, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai satta…pai॰…|

kamma-vipākadukāni

275. kammapac‍cayā  …pai॰… vipākapac‍cayā haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ, adhipatiyā aikaṃ, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai  aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai aikaṃ, puraijātai aikaṃ, kammai aikaṃ, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, jhānai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

āhārādidukāni

276. āhārapac‍cayā…pai॰… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

avigatapac‍cayā haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai satta…pai॰… vigatai satta|

pac‍cayavārai anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

277. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati na haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ  kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ … āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ … asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

278. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

(yathā paṭic‍cavārai naārammaṇapac‍cayā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

279. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (abyākataṃ paripuṇṇaṃ kātabbaṃ)| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā  kusalā khandhā…pai॰…|

(yathā anulaumai sahajātapac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

naanantarādipac‍cayā

280. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā|

(yathā paṭic‍cavārai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

napacchājātādipac‍cayā

281. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… (napacchājātapac‍cayampi naāsaivanapac‍cayampi paripuṇṇaṃ, sattarasa|)

(yathā anulaumai sahajātapac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nakammapac‍cayau

282. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai pac‍cayā kusalā caitanā| (1)

akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai pac‍cayā akusalā caitanā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā, kiriyābyākatai khandhai pac‍cayā kiriyābyākatā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… upādārūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kiriyābyākatā caitanā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā caitanā| (2)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ  pac‍cayā akusalā caitanā| (3)

kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā kusalā caitanā| (1)

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā akusalā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

283. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā  dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kiriyābyākatā khandhā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā khandhā|

(vipākaṃ ṭhapaitvā sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

naāhārādipac‍cayā

284. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā… naindriyapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| (na  jhānai idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ|) namaggapac‍cayā… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukavipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā| (namaggai idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ|)

(avasaisaṃ yathā paṭic‍cavārai pac‍canīyaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nasampayuttādipac‍cayā

285. nasampayuttapac‍cayā … navippayuttapac‍cayā… naunatthipac‍cayā… kusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nauvigatapac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

(yathā paṭic‍cavārai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

286. nahaituyā  cattāri, na ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā  pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

nahaitudukaṃ

287. nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai cattāri, naāsaivanai cattāri, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai cattāri, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, (sabbattha aikaṃ) naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

naārammaṇadukaṃ

288. naārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai pañ‍ca, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai  pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

tikaṃ

naārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… nauvigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

naadhipatidukaṃ

289. naadhipatipac‍cayā nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

tikaṃ

naadhipatipac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai cattāri, naāsaivanai cattāri, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai cattāri, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

catuk‍kaṃ

naadhipatipac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naanantarai aikaṃ, (sabbattha aikaṃ) …pai॰…|

naanantarādidukāni

290. naanantarapac‍cayā  nasamanantarapac‍cayā naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā naupanissayapac‍cayā (naārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

napuraijātadukaṃ

291. napuraijātapac‍cayā  nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai satta, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

tikaṃ

napuraijātapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā dvai, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

catuk‍kaṃ

napuraijātapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, (sabbattha aikaṃ) nauvigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

napacchājāta-naāsaivanadukāni

292. napacchājātapac‍cayā…pai॰… naāsaivanapac‍cayā nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai  aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

tikaṃ

naāsaivanapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā cattāri, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai cattāri, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai cattāri, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

catuk‍kaṃ

naāsaivanapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, (sabbattha aikaṃ) nauvigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

nakammadukaṃ

293. nakammapac‍cayā nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, navipākai satta, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā  aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

tikaṃ

nakammapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ, (sabbattha aikaṃ) nauvigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

navipākadukaṃ

294. navipākapac‍cayā  nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā naāhārapac‍cayā naindriyapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauvigatapac‍cayā sahajātai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ|

pac‍cayavārau|

4. nissayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

329. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ nissāya kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai nissāya aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai nissāya dvai khandhā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ nissāya abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ nissāya kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai nissāya aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai nissāya dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

330. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ nissāya akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ  khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai nissāya dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ nissāya abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ  nissāya akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai nissāya dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

331. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ nissāya abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai nissāya aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai nissāya dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai nissāya aikau khandhau kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandhai nissāya dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai nissāya vatthu, vatthuṃ nissāya khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ nissāya tayau mahābhūtā, tayau mahābhūtai nissāya aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, dvai mahābhūtai nissāya dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; vatthuṃ nissāya vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ nissāya kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ nissāya kusalā khandhā| (2)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ nissāya akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ nissāya akusalā khandhā| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ nissāya kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ nissāya kusalā khandhā, mahābhūtai nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ  nissāya akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ nissāya akusalā khandhā, mahābhūtai nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

332. kusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ nissāya kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya dvai khandhā| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ nissāya abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca  dhammaṃ nissāya kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya dvai khandhā, kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ nissāya akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya tayau khandhā …pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya dvai khandhā| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ nissāya abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ nissāya akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya dvai  khandhā, akusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

(yathā pac‍cayavārai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

333. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai satta, kammai sattarasa, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai sattarasa, jhānai sattarasa, maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai sattarasa|

(yathā  pac‍cayavārai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

nissayavārai anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

334. akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ nissāya akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai nissāya vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ nissāya abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, tayau khandhai nissāya aikau khandhau cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, dvai khandha nissāya dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai nissāya dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai nissāya vatthu, vatthuṃ nissāya khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ nissāya tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai nissāya cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ nissāya  tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai nissāya kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; cakkhāyatanaṃ nissāya cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ nissāya kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ nissāya ahaitukā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ nissāya akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ nissāya vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ nissāya akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca nissāya vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

(yathā pac‍cayavārai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

335. nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

nissayavārai pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

336. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai satta, navipākai sattarasa, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nissayavārai anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

337. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai cattāri, anantarai cattāri, samanantarai cattāri, sahajātai cattāri, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cattāri, nissayai cattāri, upanissayai cattāri, puraijātai cattāri, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai cattāri, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, jhānai cattāri, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai cattāri, vippayuttai cattāri, atthiyā cattāri, natthiyā cattāri, vigatai cattāri, avigatai cattāri| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nissayavārai pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

(pac‍cayattaṃ nāma nissayattaṃ, nissayattaṃ nāma pac‍cayattaṃ|)

nissayavārau|

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

338. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

ārammaṇādipac‍cayā

339. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… adhipatipac‍cayā… (adhipati paṭisandhikkhaṇai natthi|) anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā| (sabbāni padāni haitumūlakasadisāni)|

puraijātapac‍cayau

340. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ  saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau  dhammau uppaj‍jati puraijātapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

341. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau…pai॰… akusalaṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā…pai॰…|

kammapac‍cayau

342. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā…pai॰… akusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau…pai॰… abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau…pai॰…|

vipākapac‍cayau

343. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ…pai॰…|

āhārādipac‍cayā

344. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā … jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā| (imāni padāni haitupac‍cayasadisāni|)

vippayuttapac‍cayau

345. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā|

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā|

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā|

atthiādipac‍cayā

346. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā (haitupac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

347. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi|

haitudukaṃ

348. haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi| (haitumūlakaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

āsaivanadukaṃ

349. āsaivanapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

vipākadukaṃ

350. vipākapac‍cayā haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ, adhipatiyā aikaṃ, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai aikaṃ, puraijātai aikaṃ, kammai aikaṃ, āhārai  aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, jhānai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

saṃsaṭṭhavārai anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

351. akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

naadhipatiādipac‍cayā

352. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai āruppai (sabbattha) kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… abyākataṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰…|

napacchājāta-naāsaivanapac‍cayā

353. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā… tīṇi| naāsaivanapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nakammapac‍cayau

354. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| kusalai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā kusalā caitanā| (1)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| akusalai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā akusalā caitanā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā| kiriyābyākatai  khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā kiriyābyākatā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

355. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau…pai॰… akusalaṃ dhammaṃ…pai॰… abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ|

(saṃsaṭṭhavārai pac‍canīyavibhaṅgai nakammai ca navipākai ca paṭisandhi natthi; avasaisaisu sabbattha atthi|)

najhānapac‍cayau

356. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

namaggapac‍cayau

357. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

navippayuttapac‍cayau

358. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vipākābyākataṃ  kiriyābyākataṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (navippayuttai paṭisandhi natthi|) (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

359. nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, na āsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi|

nahaitudukaṃ

360. nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai|

catuk‍kaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai…pai॰…|

chak‍kaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā naāsaivanapac‍cayā nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai|

sattakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naadhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā naāsaivanapac‍cayā nakammapac‍cayā navipākai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

navakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naadhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā namaggapac‍cayā navippayuttai aikaṃ|

naadhipatidukaṃ

361. naadhipatipac‍cayā nahaituyā dvai, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi|

tikaṃ

naadhipatipac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

napuraijātadukaṃ

362. napuraijātapac‍cayā nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi|

tikaṃ

napuraijātapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

napacchājāta-naāsaivanadukāni

363. napacchājātapac‍cayā  …pai॰… naāsaivanapac‍cayā nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi|

tikaṃ

naāsaivanapac‍cayā  nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai dvai, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nakammadukaṃ

364. nakammapac‍cayā  nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi|

tikaṃ

nakammapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, napuraijātai aikaṃ, napacchājātai aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, navipākai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

navipākadukaṃ

365. navipākapac‍cayā nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰…|

sattakaṃ

namaggapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

navippayuttadukaṃ

391. navippayuttapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi|

tikaṃ

navippayuttapac‍cayā  nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai, anantarai dvai, samanantarai dvai, sahajātai dvai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai dvai, upanissayai dvai, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai dvai, āhārai dvai, indriyai dvai, jhānai dvai, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai dvai, atthiyā dvai, natthiyā dvai, vigatai dvai, avigatai dvai…pai॰…|

navakaṃ

navippayuttapac‍cayā  nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā (naāsaivanapac‍cayamūlakampi  nahaitumūlakasadisaṃ|) nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā namaggapac‍cayā (imāni tīṇi mūlāni aikasadisāni) ārammaṇai aikaṃ, anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, upanissayai aikaṃ, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, jhānai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ|

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

saṃsaṭṭhavārau|

6. sampayuttavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

392. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ sampayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sampayuttau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sampayuttā dvai khandhā| (1)

393. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ sampayuttau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sampayuttau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sampayuttā dvai khandhā| (1)

394. abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ sampayuttau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sampayuttau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sampayuttā  dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā  tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sampayuttau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sampayuttā dvai khandhā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) (1)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

395. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi|

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

396. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ sampayuttau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai sampayuttau vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

397. abyākataṃ dhammaṃ sampayuttau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sampayuttau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sampayuttā dvai khandhā; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sampayuttau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sampayuttā dvai khandhā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) (1)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

398. nahaituyā  dvai, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

399. haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

400. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai, anantarai dvai, samanantarai dvai, sahajātai dvai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai dvai, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai dvai, kammai dvai, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai dvai, indriyai dvai, jhānai dvai, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai dvai, atthiyā dvai, natthiyā dvai, vigatai dvai, avigatai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

sampayuttavārau|

(saṃsaṭṭhattaṃ nāma sampayuttattaṃ, sampayuttattaṃ nāma saṃsaṭṭhattaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

401. kusalau  dhammau kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

402. akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

403. abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

404. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, saikkhā gautrabhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, saikkhā maggā  vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā kusalaṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina kusalacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanakusalaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanakusalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakusalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa caitaupariyañāṇassa pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa yathākammūpagañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

405. kusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| pubbai suciṇṇāni assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha  rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| jhānai parihīnai vippaṭisārissa daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

406. kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arahā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati; pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati; kusalaṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vippassati; caitaupariyañāṇaina kusalacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā kusalaṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, kusalai niruddhai vipākau  tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| kusalaṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, akusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanakusalaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanavipākassa ca, kiriyassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanavipākassa ca, kiriyassa ca ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

407. akusalau  dhammau akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| diṭṭhiṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vicikicchaṃ ārabbha vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| daumanassaṃ ārabbha daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati| (1)

408. akusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saikkhā pahīnai kilaisai pahīnakilaisai (syā॰) pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai vikkhambhitakilaisai (syā॰) pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti, saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā akusalaṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina akusalacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| akusalā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

409. akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arahā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānāti, akusalaṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina akusalacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti, saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā akusalaṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, kusalai niruddhai  vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| akusalaṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| akusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| akusalā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa yathākammūpagañāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

410. abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arahā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| nibbānaṃ phalassa āvaj‍janāya  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arahā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina vipākābyākatakiriyābyākatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanakiriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanakiriyassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanakiriyaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakiriyassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa… gandhāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇassa… rasāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa … phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

411. abyākatau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saikkhā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa vaudānassa maggassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vippassanti| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passanti, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina vipākābyākatakiriyābyākatacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| abyākatā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa  caitaupariyañāṇassa pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

412. abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau  uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

413. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā, taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| saikkhā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – kusalādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

414. kusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā, taṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati|

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – arahā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sahajātādhipati  – kusalādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – kusalādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

415. akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| diṭṭhiṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau  uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – akusalādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – akusalādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa adhipati pac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – akusalādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

416. abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – arahā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| nibbānaṃ phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – vipākābyākatakiriyābyākatādhipati  sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – saikkhā  phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

417. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā kusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… maggau phalassa… anulaumaṃ saikkhāya phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ  phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā akusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vipākābyākatānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ  khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… arahatau anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakiriyaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… maggau phalassa… anulaumaṃ saikkhāya phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā  vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ phalasamāpattiyā samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā akusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)
