sahajātapac‍cayau

419. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa  cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – akusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatau kiriyābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bāhiraṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa  sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā  dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau  ca abyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

420. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatau kiriyābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nissayapac‍cayau

421. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau  dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau  khandhā aikassa khandhassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau  khandhā aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – vipākābyākatau kiriyābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ, kaṭattārūpānaṃ, upādārūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā kaṭattārūpānaṃ, upādārūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… ghānāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ  …pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu vipākābyākatānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

abyākatau  dhammau kusalassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

422. kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā kusalassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau  aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā ca vatthu ca aikassa khandhassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā akusalassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā ca vatthu ca aikassa khandhassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau  ca abyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

upanissayapac‍cayau

423. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

ārammaṇūpanissayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā, taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, saikkhā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti|

anantarūpanissayau  – purimā purimā kusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau  – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… sutaṃ…pai॰… cāgaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissaya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā … saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dutiyassa jhānassa parikammaṃ dutiyassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tatiyassa jhānassa parikammaṃ tatiyassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| catutthassa jhānassa parikammaṃ catutthassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa parikammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ catutthassa jhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dibbassa cakkhussa parikammaṃ dibbassa cakkhussa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dibbāya sautadhātuyā parikammaṃ dibbāya sautadhātuyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| iddhividhañāṇassa parikammaṃ iddhividhañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| caitaupariyañāṇassa parikammaṃ caitaupariyañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa  parikammaṃ pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| yathākammūpagañāṇassa parikammaṃ yathākammūpagañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anāgataṃsañāṇassa parikammaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dibbacakkhu dibbāya sautadhātuyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dibbasautadhātu iddhividhañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| iddhividhañāṇaṃ caitaupariyañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| caitaupariyañāṇaṃ pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ yathākammūpagañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| yathākammūpagañāṇaṃ anāgataṃsañāṇassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamassa maggassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dutiyassa maggassa parikammaṃ dutiyassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tatiyassa maggassa parikammaṃ tatiyassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| catutthassa maggassa parikammaṃ catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dutiyau maggau tatiyassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ samāpattiṃ uppādainti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍janti, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti| maggau saikkhānaṃ atthappaṭisambhidāya, dhammappaṭisambhidāya, niruttippaṭisambhidāya, paṭibhānappaṭisambhidāya, ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

ārammaṇūpanissayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati|

pakatūpanissayau  – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… sutaṃ…pai॰… cāgaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

ārammaṇūpanissayau – arahā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati|

anantarūpanissayau – kusalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… maggau phalassa… anulaumaṃ saikkhāya phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau  – saddhaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| sīlaṃ…pai॰… sutaṃ…pai॰… cāgaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalaṃ kammaṃ vipākassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arahā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kiriyasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍jati, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| maggau arahatau atthappaṭisambhidāya, dhammappaṭisambhidāya, niruttippaṭisambhidāya, paṭibhānappaṭisambhidāya, ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

ārammaṇūpanissayau – rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| diṭṭhiṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati|

anantarūpanissayau – purimā purimā akusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau  – rāgaṃ  upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati, adin‍naṃ ādiyati, musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, pharusaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati, sandhiṃ  chindati, nil‍laupaṃ harati, aikāgārikaṃ karauti, paripanthai tiṭṭhati, paradāraṃ gacchati, gāmaghātaṃ karauti, nigamaghātaṃ karauti, mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, arahantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, duṭṭhaina cittaina tathāgatassa lauhitaṃ uppādaiti, saṅghaṃ bhindati| dausaṃ upanissāya…pai॰… mauhaṃ upanissāya…pai॰… mānaṃ upanissāya…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ upanissāya…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pāṇātipātau pāṇātipātassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pāṇātipātau adin‍nādānassa…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārassa…pai॰… musāvādassa…pai॰… pisuṇāya vācāya…pai॰… pharusāya vācāya…pai॰… samphappalāpassa…pai॰… abhijjhāya…pai॰… byāpādassa…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adin‍nādānaṃ adin‍nādānassa… kāmaisumicchācārassa… musāvādassa… (saṃkhittaṃ) micchādiṭṭhiyā… pāṇātipātassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (cak‍kaṃ bandhitabbaṃ|) kāmaisumicchācārau…pai॰… musāvādau…pai॰… pisuṇavācā…pai॰… pharusavācā…pai॰… samphappalāpau…pai॰… abhijjhā…pai॰… byāpādau…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| micchādiṭṭhi pāṇātipātassa… adin‍nādānassa… kāmaisumicchācārassa… musāvādassa… pisuṇāya vācāya… pharusāya vācāya… samphappalāpassa… abhijjhāya… byāpādassa upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| mātughātikammaṃ mātughātikammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mātughātikammaṃ pitughātikammassa upanissaya…pai॰… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa… saṅghabhaidakammassa… niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pitughātikammaṃ pitughātikammassa… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa… saṅghabhaidakammassa… niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā… mātughātikammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arahantaghātikammaṃ arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa…pai॰… ruhiruppādakammaṃ ruhiruppādakammassa…pai॰… saṅghabhaidakammaṃ saṅghabhaidakammassa…pai॰… niyatamicchādiṭṭhi niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| niyatamicchādiṭṭhi mātughātikammassa upanissaya…pai॰… arahantaghātikammassa… ruhiruppādakammassa…  saṅghabhaidakammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|) (1)

akusalau  dhammau kusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| dausaṃ…pai॰… mauhaṃ…pai॰… mānaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| rāgau… dausau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa … pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pāṇaṃ hantvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| adin‍naṃ ādiyitvā…pai॰… musā bhaṇitvā…pai॰… pisuṇaṃ bhaṇitvā…pai॰… pharusaṃ bhaṇitvā…pai॰… samphaṃ palapitvā…pai॰… sandhiṃ chinditvā…pai॰… nil‍laupaṃ haritvā…pai॰… aikāgārikaṃ karitvā…pai॰… paripanthai ṭhatvā…pai॰… paradāraṃ gantvā…pai॰… gāmaghātaṃ karitvā…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karitvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā…pai॰… arahantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā…pai॰… duṭṭhaina cittaina tathāgatassa lauhitaṃ uppādaitvā…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhinditvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| (2)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

anantarūpanissayau – akusalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| dausaṃ…pai॰… mauhaṃ…pai॰… mānaṃ …pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ  ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| rāgau… dausau … mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā kāyikassa sukhassa… kāyikassa dukkhassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalaṃ kammaṃ vipākassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

ārammaṇūpanissayau – arahā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, nibbānaṃ phalassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

anantarūpanissayau – purimā purimā vipākābyākatā, kiriyābyākatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vipākābyākatānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… arahatau anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakiriyaṃ phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau – kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| utu kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bhaujanaṃ kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saināsanaṃ kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa  dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phalasamāpatti kāyikassa sukhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

arahā kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kiriyasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍jati, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| kāyikaṃ  dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ … saināsanaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kiriyasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍jati, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| (1)

abyākatau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

ārammaṇūpanissayau – saikkhā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa… vaudānassa… maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

anantarūpanissayau – āvaj‍janā kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau – kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ  uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau|

ārammaṇūpanissayau – cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ…pai॰… rūpai…pai॰… saddai…pai॰… gandhai…pai॰… rasai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…pai॰… vatthuṃ…pai॰… vipākābyākatai kiriyābyākatai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati|

anantarūpanissayau  – āvaj‍janā akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau – kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati, adin‍naṃ ādiyati, musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, pharusaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati, sandhiṃ  chindati, nil‍laupaṃ harati, aikāgārikaṃ karauti, paripanthai tiṭṭhati, paradāraṃ gacchati, gāmaghātaṃ karauti, nigamaghātaṃ karauti, mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, arahantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, duṭṭhaina cittaina tathāgatassa lauhitaṃ uppādaiti, saṅghaṃ bhindati|

kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… utuṃ…pai॰… bhaujanaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati… (saṃkhittaṃ|) saṅghaṃ bhindati|

kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ… utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ rāgassa… dausassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

puraijātapac‍cayau

424. abyākatau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ|

ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – arahā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ…pai॰… rūpai…pai॰… saddai…pai॰… gandhai…pai॰… rasai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saddāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… gandhāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… ghānāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu vipākābyākatānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ|

ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ  – saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti| sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ…pai॰… rūpai…pai॰… saddai…pai॰… gandhai…pai॰… rasai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti| dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passanti| dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇanti|

vatthupuraijātaṃ  – vatthu kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ…pai॰… rūpai…pai॰… saddai…pai॰… gandhai…pai॰… rasai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati|

vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

425. kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā kusalā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā akusalā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

426. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – purimā purimā kusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ  āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā akusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā kiriyābyākatā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

427. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā nānākhaṇikā (ka॰)| sahajātā – kusalā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

nānākkhaṇikā  – kusalā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau  dhammau akusalassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – akusalā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – akusalā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau  dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ, cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| caitanā vatthussa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

428. abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa  khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatau  aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

āhārapac‍cayau

429. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā āhārā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau  dhammau akusalassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā āhārā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

indriyapac‍cayau

430. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā indriyā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – akusalā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā indriyā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau  dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautindriyaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa  …pai॰… ghānindriyaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… jivhindriyaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

jhānapac‍cayau

431. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalāni jhānaṅgāni cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatāni kiriyābyākatāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatāni jhānaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalāni maggaṅgāni sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

sampayuttapac‍cayau

433. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau  tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau  khandhā aikassa khandhassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākābyākatau kiriyābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vippayuttapac‍cayau

434. kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – kusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – kusalā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – akusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – akusalā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ghānāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| jivhāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu vipākābyākatānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ  khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ vatthu kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – puraijātaṃ vatthu akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

atthipac‍cayau

435. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – kusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – kusalā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kusalau dhammau kusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa  khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

akusalau  dhammau akusalassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau  dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – akusalā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – akusalā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – akusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

abyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – vipākābyākatau kiriyābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākābyākatau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau mahābhūtā aikassa mahābhūtassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai mahābhūtā dvin‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mahābhūtā kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

puraijātaṃ  – arahā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati… sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ…pai॰… rūpai…pai॰… saddai…pai॰… gandhai…pai॰… rasai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai  …pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati; dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sautāyatanaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… ghānāyatanaṃ ghānaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu vipākābyākatānaṃ kiriyābyākatānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākābyākatā kiriyābyākatā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti… sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ…pai॰… rūpai…pai॰… saddai…pai॰… gandhai…pai॰… rasai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passanti, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇanti| vatthu kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

abyākatau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – puraijātaṃ cakkhuṃ assādaiti, abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ…pai॰… rūpai…pai॰… saddai…pai॰… gandhai…pai॰… rasai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…pai॰… vatthuṃ assādaiti, abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthu akusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā kusalassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – kusalau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau  ca abyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – kusalā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – kusalā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā kusalā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā akusalassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – akusalau aikau  khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā ca vatthu ca aikassa khandhassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā ca vatthu ca dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – akusalā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – akusalā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – akusalā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthipac‍cayau

436. kusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā kusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ)

(yathā anantarapac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

vigatapac‍cayau

437. kusalau  dhammau kusalassa dhammassa vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā kusalā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kusalānaṃ khandhānaṃ vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ)

(yathā anantarapac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

avigatapac‍cayau

438. kusalau  dhammau kusalassa dhammassa avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – kusalau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tayau khandhā aikassa khandhassa avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ)

(yathā atthipac‍cayaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

pañhāvārassa vibhaṅgau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

439. haituyā satta, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa|

haitusabhāgaṃ

440. haitupac‍cayā  adhipatiyā cattāri, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, indriyai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, avigatai satta| (11)

haitusāmañ‍ñaghaṭanā (9)

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti  aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ñanissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-sampayuttaatthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| (savipākaṃ – 5)

saindriyamaggaghaṭanā (9)

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajātaañ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ñanissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipākaindriya-magga-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipākaindriya-magga-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| (savipākaṃ – 5)

sādhipati-indriya-maggaghaṭanā (6)

haitādhipati-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitādhipatisahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| haitādhipati-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| (savipākaṃ – 3)

kammamūlakaṃ|

vipākasabhāgaṃ

476. vipākapac‍cayā haituyā aikaṃ, adhipatiyā aikaṃ, sahajātai aikaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nissayai aikaṃ, kammai aikaṃ, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, jhānai aikaṃ, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ| (14)

vipākaghaṭanā (5)

477. vipāka-sahajāta-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti  aikaṃ| vipāka-sahajātaañ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| vipāka-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-sampayuttaatthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| vipāka-sahajāta-nissaya-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| vipāka-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

532. nahaituyā pan‍narasa, naārammaṇai pan‍narasa, naadhipatiyā pan‍narasa, naanantarai pan‍narasa, nasamanantarai pan‍narasa, nasahajātai aikādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikādasa, nanissayai aikādasa, naupanissayai pan‍narasa, napuraijātai tairasa, napacchājātai pan‍narasa, naāsaivanai pan‍narasa, nakammai pan‍narasa, navipākai pan‍narasa, naāhārai pan‍narasa, naindriyai pan‍narasa, najhānai pan‍narasa, namaggai pan‍narasa, nasampayuttai aikādasa, navippayuttai  nava, nauatthiyā nava, naunatthiyā pan‍narasa, nauvigatai pan‍narasa, nauavigatai nava|

nahaitudukaṃ

533. nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pan‍narasa, naadhipatiyā pan‍narasa, naanantarai pan‍narasa, nasamanantarai pan‍narasa, nasahajātai aikādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikādasa, nanissayai aikādasa, naupanissayai pan‍narasa, napuraijātai tairasa, napacchājātai pan‍narasa, naāsaivanai pan‍narasa, nakammai pan‍narasa, navipākai pan‍narasa, naāhārai pan‍narasa, naindriyai pan‍narasa, najhānai pan‍narasa, namaggai pan‍narasa, nasampayuttai aikādasa, navippayuttai nava, nauatthiyā nava, naunatthiyā pan‍narasa, nauvigatai pan‍narasa, nauavigatai nava|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā pan‍narasa, naanantarai pan‍narasa, nasamanantarai pan‍narasa, nasahajātai aikādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikādasa, nanissayai aikādasa, naupanissayai tairasa, napuraijātai tairasa, napacchājātai pan‍narasa, naāsaivanai pan‍narasa, nakammai pan‍narasa, navipākai pan‍narasa, naāhārai pan‍narasa, naindriyai pan‍narasa, najhānai pan‍narasa, namaggai pan‍narasa, nasampayuttai aikādasa, navippayuttai nava, nauatthiyā nava, naunatthiyā pan‍narasa, nauvigatai pan‍narasa, nauavigatai nava…pai॰…|

chak‍kaṃ

pañhāvārassa pac‍canīyagaṇanā|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

550. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai  satta, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai satta, navipākai satta, naāhārai satta, naindriyai satta, najhānai satta, namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta| (19)

haitusāmañ‍ñaghaṭanā (9)

551. haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai satta, navipākai satta, naāhārai satta, naindriyai satta, najhānai satta, namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta|

haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi, naindriyai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

haitu  -sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi, naindriyai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi, naindriyai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, nasampayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi| (avipākaṃ – 4)

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti  naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai aikaṃ, napacchājātai aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai aikaṃ, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai aikaṃ, napacchājātai  aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai aikaṃ, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

haitumūlakaṃ|

ārammaṇadukaṃ

554. ārammaṇapac‍cayā nahaituyā nava, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai nava, nasamanantarai nava, nasahajātai nava, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nanissayai nava, naupanissayai nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai nava, navipākai nava, naāhārai nava, naindriyai nava, najhānai nava, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai nava, navippayuttai nava, nauatthiyā nava, naunatthiyā nava, nauvigatai nava, nauavigatai nava| (23)

ārammaṇaghaṭanā (5)

555. ārammaṇa-adhipati-upanissayanti  nahaituyā satta, naanantarai sattaṃ, nasamanantarai satta, nasahajātai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai satta, navipākai satta, naāhārai satta, naindriyai satta, najhānai satta, namaggai satta, nasampayuttai satta, navippayuttai satta, nauatthiyā satta, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta, nauavigatai satta|

ārammaṇa  -puraijāta-atthi-avigatanti nahaituyā tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, nasahajātai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nanissayai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi, naindriyai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

ārammaṇa-nissaya-puraijāta-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti nahaituyā tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, nasahajātai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi, naindriyai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, nasampayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

ārammaṇa-adhipati-upanissaya-puraijāta-atthi-avigatanti nahaituyā aikaṃ, naanantarai aikaṃ, nasamanantarai aikaṃ, nasahajātai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aikaṃ, nanissayai aikaṃ, napacchājātai aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai aikaṃ, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā aikaṃ, nauvigatai aikaṃ|

ārammaṇamūlakaṃ|

adhipatidukaṃ

556. adhipatipac‍cayā nahaituyā dasa, naārammaṇai satta, naanantarai dasa, nasamanantarai dasa, nasahajātai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai aṭṭha, nanissayai satta, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai dasa, napacchājātai dasa, naāsaivanai dasa, nakammai dasa, navipākai dasa, naāhārai dasa, naindriyai dasa, najhānai dasa, namaggai dasa, nasampayuttai aṭṭha, navippayuttai satta, nauatthiyā satta, naunatthiyā dasa, nauvigatai dasa, nau avigatai satta| (23)

adhipatimissakaghaṭanā (3)

557. adhipati-atthi-avigatanti nahaituyā aṭṭha, naārammaṇai satta, naanantarai aṭṭha, nasamanantarai aṭṭha, nasahajātai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai cattāri, nanissayai aikaṃ, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai aṭṭha, naāsaivanai aṭṭha, nakammai aṭṭha, navipākai aṭṭha, naāhārai aṭṭha, naindriyai aṭṭha, najhānai aṭṭha, namaggai aṭṭha, nasampayuttai cattāri, navippayuttai cattāri, naunatthiyā aṭṭha, nauvigatai aṭṭha|

adhipati  -nissaya-atthi-avigatanti nahaituyā aṭṭha, naārammaṇai satta, naanantarai aṭṭha, nasamanantarai aṭṭha, nasahajātai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai cattāri, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai aṭṭha, naāsaivanai aṭṭha, nakammai aṭṭha, navipākai aṭṭha, naāhārai aṭṭha, naindriyai aṭṭha, najhānai aṭṭha, namaggai aṭṭha, nasampayuttai cattāri, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā aṭṭha, nauvigatai aṭṭha|

adhipati-nissaya-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti  nahaituyā cattāri, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naanantarai cattāri, nasamanantarai cattāri, nasahajātai aikaṃ, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai cattāri, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai cattāri, naāsaivanai cattāri, nakammai cattāri, navipākai cattāri, naāhārai cattāri, naindriyai cattāri, najhānai cattāri, namaggai cattāri, nasampayuttai cattāri, naunatthiyā cattāri, nauvigatai cattāri|

pakiṇṇakaghaṭanā (3)

natthi-vigatadukāni

629. natthipac‍cayā nahaituyā satta…pai॰… vigatapac‍cayā nahaituyā satta…pai॰…| (natthipac‍cayampi vigatapac‍cayampi anantarapac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

avigatadukaṃ

630. avigatapac‍cayā nahaituyā tairasa…| (yathā atthipac‍cayau vitthāritau aivaṃ avigatapac‍cayau vitthāraitabbau|)

pañhāvārassa anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

631. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatiyā satta, anantarai  satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa|

catuk‍kaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa…pai॰…|

chak‍kaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai  tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, avigatai tairasa|

sattakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā nasahajātapac‍cayā nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, kammai dvai, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā satta, avigatai satta|

aṭṭhakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā nasahajātapac‍cayā naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, kammai dvai, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā satta, avigatai satta|

navakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā nanissayapac‍cayā upanissayai nava, pacchājātai tīṇi, kammai dvai, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā pañ‍ca, avigatai pañ‍ca…pai॰…|

aikādasakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā (mūlakaṃ saṃkhittaṃ) nanissayapac‍cayā naupanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā pacchājātai tīṇi, kammai  dvai, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā pañ‍ca, avigatai pañ‍ca|

dvādasakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā (mūlakaṃ saṃkhittaṃ) naupanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā kammai dvai, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

sauḷasakaṃ (sāhāraṃ)

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā (mūlakaṃ saṃkhittaṃ) napacchājātapac‍cayā naāsaivanapac‍cayā nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā naāhārapac‍cayā indriyai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

bāvīsakaṃ (sāhāraṃ)

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā (mūlakaṃ saṃkhittaṃ) naāhārapac‍cayā najhānapac‍cayā namaggapac‍cayā nasampayuttapac‍cayā navippayuttapac‍cayā naunatthipac‍cayā nauvigatapac‍cayā indriyai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ|

nasampayuttapac‍cayā  nahaitupac‍cayā (mūlakaṃ saṃkhittaṃ) nanissayapac‍cayā naupanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā pacchājātai tīṇi, kammai dvai, āhārai aikaṃ, indriyai aikaṃ, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā pañ‍ca, avigatai pañ‍ca (saṃkhittaṃ)|

navippayuttadukaṃ

649. navippayuttapac‍cayā  haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai pañ‍ca, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā pañ‍ca, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai pañ‍ca…pai॰…|

catuk‍kaṃ

2. vaidanāttikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

ārammaṇapac‍cayādi

2. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… adhipatipac‍cayā  (adhipatiyā paṭisandhi natthi)… anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

āsaivanapac‍cayādi

3. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā… kammapac‍cayā… vipākapac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca  aikau khandhau|

āhārapac‍cayādi

4. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau  khandhau; vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati vippayuttapac‍cayā – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| vatthuṃ vippayuttapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

atthipac‍cayādi

5. atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā…|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

6. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

haituādidukāni

7. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… vipākai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

ārammaṇapac‍cayā  adhipatipac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… vipākai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

āsaivanapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… kammai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

vipākapac‍cayā haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… puraijātai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi…pai॰… jhānai dvai, maggai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

8. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati  nahaitupac‍cayā – dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau|

naadhipatipac‍cayau

9. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (nādhipati paripuṇṇaṃ paṭisandhikaṃ)|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

10. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai  khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

napacchājāta-naāsaivanapac‍cayā

11. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati  napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā (napacchājātampi naāsaivanampi paripuṇṇaṃ paṭisandhikaṃ)|

nakammapac‍cayau

12. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā|

dukkhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā|

navipāka-najhānapac‍cayā

13. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā – sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai  khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – catuviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

namaggapac‍cayau

14. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

navippayuttapac‍cayau

15. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

16. nahaituyā tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai  tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai|

nahaitudukaṃ

17. nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai aikaṃ, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, navippayuttai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

catuk‍kaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātai aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

navakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naadhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā naāsaivanapac‍cayā nakammapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā namaggapac‍cayā navippayuttai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatidukaṃ

18. naadhipatipac‍cayā  nahaituyā tīṇi, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

napuraijātadukaṃ

19. napuraijātapac‍cayā nahaituyā aikaṃ, naadhipatiyā dvai, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai dvai|

tikaṃ

napuraijātapac‍cayā  nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā aikaṃ, napacchājātai aikaṃ, naāsaivanai aikaṃ, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai aikaṃ, namaggai aikaṃ, navippayuttai aikaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)|

napacchājātādidukāni

20. napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā… nakammapac‍cayā nahaituyā dvai, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai dvai, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, navipākai tīṇi, namaggai dvai, navippayuttai dvai|

tikaṃ

nakammapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā dvai, napuraijātai aikaṃ, napacchājātai dvai, naāsaivanai dvai, navipākai dvai, namaggai dvai, navippayuttai aikaṃ…pai॰…|

najhānadukaṃ

pac‍canīyagaṇanā|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau
