6. sampayuttavārau

37. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ sampayuttau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā dvai khandhā, dvai khandhai sampayuttau aikau khandhau (saṃkhittaṃ)|

sampayuttavārau|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

38. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

39. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā vippaṭisārissa daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai  jhānai parihīnai vippaṭisārissa daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (2)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, caitaupariyañāṇaina sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

40. dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – dausaṃ ārabbha dausau uppaj‍jati, mauhau uppaj‍jati; dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ mauhaṃ ārabbha mauhau uppaj‍jati, dausau uppaj‍jati; dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ārabbha  dausau uppaj‍jati, mauhau uppaj‍jati; dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati; dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (2)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai  pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati; caitaupariyañāṇaina dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

41. adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati; ariyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai  pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina  cittaina  anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati; caitaupariyañāṇaina adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina pac‍cavaikkhati| ariyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (2)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā vippaṭisārissa daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttai jhānai parihīnai vippaṭisārissa daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai ārabbha dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā uppaj‍janti| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

42. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati|

ārammaṇādhipati – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati|

sahajātādhipati – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

ārammaṇādhipati – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

43. dukkhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

sahajātādhipati – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

44. adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati|

ārammaṇādhipati – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati|

sahajātādhipati – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

ārammaṇādhipati – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā, maggā vuṭṭhahitvā, phalā vuṭṭhahitvā  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina garuṃ katvā assādaiti  abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

anantarapac‍cayau

45. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ  anulaumaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa gautrabhussa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ cuticittaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  vipākamanaudhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu kiriyamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa bhavaṅgassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalākusalaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa vuṭṭhānassa… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

46. dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dukkhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipākamanaudhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

47. adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ anulaumaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa gautrabhussa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa… maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa… anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ cuticittaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa upapatticittassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āvaj‍janā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākamanaudhātu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa bhavaṅgassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalākusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa vuṭṭhānassa… kiriyaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… phalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayau

48. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarapac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

49. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

50. dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttapaṭisandhi na labbhati|) (1)

51. adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā aikassa khandhassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissayapac‍cayā

52. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (añ‍ñamañ‍ñampi nissayampi sahajātapac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

53. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ  uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ… sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti (saddhāpañ‍camakaisu ‘‘mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhātī’’ti kātabbaṃ, avasaisaisu  na kātabbaṃ)| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati, musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati, sandhiṃ chindati, nil‍laupaṃ harati, aikāgārikaṃ karauti, paripanthai tiṭṭhati, paradāraṃ gacchati, gāmaghātaṃ karauti, nigamaghātaṃ karauti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā… sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya… sukhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ rāgaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ… sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati, musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, pharusaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati, sandhiṃ chindati, nil‍laupaṃ harati, aikāgārikaṃ karauti, paripanthai  tiṭṭhati, paradāraṃ gacchati, gāmaghātaṃ karauti, nigamaghātaṃ karauti, mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, arahantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, duṭṭhaina cittaina tathāgatassa lauhitaṃ uppādaiti, saṅghaṃ bhindati| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā… sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dausassa… mauhassa… dukkhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ… sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati, musā bhaṇati, pisuṇaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati pharusaṃ bhaṇati, samphaṃ palapati (sī॰ syā॰), sandhiṃ chindati, nil‍laupaṃ harati, aikāgārikaṃ karauti, paripanthai tiṭṭhati, paradāraṃ gacchati, gāmaghātaṃ karauti, nigamaghātaṃ karauti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā… sukhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya saddhāya… sīlassa… sutassa… cāgassa… pañ‍ñāya… rāgassa… mauhassa… mānassa… diṭṭhiyā… patthanāya… adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

54. dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau  – dausaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| mauhaṃ, dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| dausau… mauhau… dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dausassa… mauhassa… dukkhasahagatassa  kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pakatūpanissayau – dausaṃ  upanissāya sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| mauhaṃ, dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| dausau… mauhau… dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya saddhāya…pai॰… sukhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – dausaṃ upanissāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| mauhaṃ, dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upanissāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| dausau… mauhau… dukkhasahagataṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya… adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

55. adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… rāgaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā saddhā… sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgau… pañ‍ñā… rāgau… mauhau… mānau… diṭṭhi… patthanā… adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina  adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… nigamaghātaṃ karauti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttāya saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya… sukhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ saddhaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti  paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ sīlaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaina cittaina adin‍naṃ ādiyati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā saddhā…pai॰… patthanā… dausassa… mauhassa… dukkhasahagatassa kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa… dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

56. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu  maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

57. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā nānākhaṇikā (ka॰)| sahajātā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā vipākānaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā  – sukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttā caitanā  vipākānaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā vipākānaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

58. dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā vipākānaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā vipākānaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

59. adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā  – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā vipākānaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā vipākānaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā caitanā vipākānaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākapac‍cayau

60. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā aikassa khandhassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai khandhā aikassa khandhassa…pai॰…| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  …pai॰…| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya…pai॰… vipākau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau aikau khandhau dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

āhārapac‍cayādi

61. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

62. haituyā  tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai aṭṭha, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tīṇi|

haitusabhāgaṃ

63. haitupac‍cayā adhipatiyā dvai, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, vipākai dvai, indriyai dvai, maggai dvai, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (10)

sāmañ‍ñaghaṭanā (2)

haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti tīṇi| haitu-sahajātaañ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti  dvai|

saindriya-maggaghaṭanā (2)

64. haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai|

sādhipati-indriya-maggaghaṭanā (2)

65. haitu-adhipati-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti  dvai haitu-adhipati-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai|

ārammaṇasabhāgaṃ

66. ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatiyā cattāri, upanissayai cattāri| (2)

ārammaṇaghaṭanā (1)

67. ārammaṇādhipati-upanissayanti cattāri|

adhipatisabhāgaṃ

68. adhipatipac‍cayā  haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai cattāri, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai cattāri, vipākai dvai, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (13)

pakiṇṇakaghaṭanā (1)

69. adhipati-ārammaṇa-upanissayanti cattāri|

sahajātaghaṭanā (8)

adhipati-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti tīṇi| adhipati-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai|

adhipati-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-āhāra-indriya-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti tīṇi| adhipati-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-āhāra-indriyasampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai|

adhipati-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti  tīṇi| adhipati-sahajātaṃ-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai|

adhipati-haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-indriya-magga  -sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai| adhipati-haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-indriya-magga-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai|

anantarasabhāgaṃ

70. anantarapac‍cayā samanantarai satta, upanissayai satta, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai dvai, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta| (6)

ghaṭanā (3)

71. anantara-samanantara-upanissaya-natthi-vigatanti satta| anantara-samanantara-upanissaya-āsaivana-natthi-vigatanti tīṇi| anantara-samanantaraupanissaya-kamma-natthi-vigatanti dvai|

samanantarapac‍cayā (anantarasadisaṃ)|

sahajātādisabhāgaṃ

72. sahajātapac‍cayā … añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… nissayapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai  tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (13)

ghaṭanā (2)

nissaya-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti tīṇi| nissaya-sahajātaañ‍ñamañ‍ña-vipāka-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti tīṇi|

upanissayasabhāgaṃ

73. upanissayapac‍cayā  ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai aṭṭha, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta| (8)

ghaṭanā (5)

74. upanissaya-ārammaṇa-adhipatīti cattāri| upanissaya-anantarasamanantara-natthi-vigatanti satta| upanissaya-anantara-samanantara-āsaivana-natthi-vigatanti tīṇi| upanissaya-kammanti aṭṭha| upanissaya-anantara-samanantara-kamma-natthi-vigatanti dvai|

āsaivanasabhāgaṃ

75. āsaivanapac‍cayā anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi| (5)

ghaṭanā (1)

āsaivana-anantara-samanantara-upanissaya-natthi-vigatanti tīṇi|

kammasabhāgaṃ

sahajātaghaṭanā (2)

vipākasabhāgaṃ

77. vipākapac‍cayā haituyā dvai, adhipatiyā dvai, sahajātai tīṇi…pai॰… jhānai dvai, maggai dvai, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (13)

sampayuttasabhāgaṃ

82. sampayuttapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (13)

ghaṭanā (2)

sampayutta-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti  tīṇi| sampayutta-sahajātaañ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti tīṇi|

(atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā…pai॰…|)

pañhāvārassa anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

83. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau,… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

84. dukkhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

85. adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

86. nahaituyā  nava, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā nava, naanantarai nava, nasamanantarai nava, nasahajātai nava, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nanissayai nava, naupanissayai nava, napuraijātai nava, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai nava, navipākai nava, naāhārai nava, naindriyai nava, najhānai nava, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai nava, navippayuttai nava, nauatthiyā nava, naunatthiyā nava, nauvigatai nava, nauavigatai nava|

nahaitudukaṃ

87. nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai nava…pai॰… nauavigatai nava|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā nava…pai॰… naupanissayai aṭṭha…pai॰… nauavigatai nava…pai॰…|

taivīsakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… naupanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā naāsaivanapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā naāhārapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauavigatai aṭṭha|

nahaitumūlakaṃ|

(yathā kusalattikassa pac‍canīyagaṇanā gaṇitā, aivaṃ idampi asammuyhantaina sabbaṃ mūlakaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ gahaitabbaṃ (syā॰)|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitusabhāgaṃ

88. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai  tīṇi, naāhārai tīṇi, naindriyai tīṇi, najhānai tīṇi, namaggai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

sāmañ‍ñaghaṭanā

89. haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikassa anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā sajjhāyamaggaina gaṇitā, aivaṃ idampi gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

kammapac‍cayā nahaituyā aṭṭha, naārammaṇai aṭṭha…pai॰… nauavigatai aṭṭha (saṃkhittaṃ)|

anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

90. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai aṭṭha, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tīṇi|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai satta…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

chak‍kaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai nava, kammai aṭṭha, vipākai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

navakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā (mūlakaṃ saṃkhittaṃ) nanissayapac‍cayā  upanissayai nava, kammai aṭṭha…pai॰…|

catuvīsakaṃ (saupanissayaṃ)

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… naupanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā naāsaivanapac‍cayā navipākapac‍cayā naāhārapac‍cayā naindriyapac‍cayā najhānapac‍cayā namaggapac‍cayā nasampayuttapac‍cayā navippayuttapac‍cayā nauatthipac‍cayā naunatthipac‍cayā nauvigatapac‍cayā nauavigatapac‍cayā kammai aṭṭha|

catuvīsakaṃ (sakammaṃ)

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… nanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātapac‍cayā  naāsaivanapac‍cayā nakammapac‍cayā…pai॰… nauvigatapac‍cayā nauavigatapac‍cayā upanissayai nava|

nahaitumūlakaṃ|

naārammaṇadukaṃ

91. naārammaṇapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… kammai aṭṭha…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

nauavigatadukaṃ

92. nauavigatapac‍cayā  ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā cattāri, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai aṭṭha, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta…pai॰…|

catuk‍kaṃ

nauavigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, upanissayai nava, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai aṭṭha, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta…pai॰…|

catuvīsakaṃ (saupanissayaṃ)

nauavigatapac‍cayā nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇa-naadhipati-naanantara-nasamanantaranasahajāta-naañ‍ñamañ‍ña-nanissaya-naupanissaya-napuraijāta-napacchājāta-naāsaivana-navipākanaāhāra-naindriya-najhāna-namagga-nasampayutta-navippayutta-nauatthipac‍cayā naunatthipac‍cayā nauvigatapac‍cayā kammai aṭṭha|

catuvīsakaṃ (sakammaṃ)

nauavigatapac‍cayā  nahaitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nanissayapac‍cayā napuraijātapac‍cayā…pai॰… nakammapac‍cayā…pai॰…  nauvigatapac‍cayā upanissayai nava|

(yathā kusalattikassa pac‍canīyānulaumagaṇanā sajjhāyamaggaina gaṇitā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

vaidanāttikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. vipākattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu| (2)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (3)

2. vipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau  ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

3. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

4. vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā  – vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

5. vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

6. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

7. vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

8. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā| (2)

9. vipākañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

10. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca tīṇi|

11. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

12. vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

13. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… (ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ) sahajātapac‍cayā… (sahajātaṃ sabbaṃ haitupac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ (sahajātai idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ)|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

14. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu| (2)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vatthu ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā vatthu ca| (3)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

15. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… aikaṃ  mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā| (2)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

nissayapac‍cayādi

16. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nissayapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)… upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā|

āsaivanapac‍cayau

17. vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai  khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā|

kamma-vipākapac‍cayā

18. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)… vipākapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vipākapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā…pai॰… naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā| vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti vipākapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

āhārapac‍cayādi

19. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā … vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

20. haituyā  tairasa, ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai tairasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai pañ‍ca, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai tairasa, vipākai nava, āhārai tairasa, indriyai tairasa, jhānai tairasa, maggai tairasa, sampayuttai pañ‍ca, vippayuttai tairasa, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā pañ‍ca, vigatai pañ‍ca, avigatai tairasa|

haitudukaṃ

21. haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai tairasa…pai॰…|

(yathā kusalattikassa gaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

āsaivanadukaṃ

22. āsaivanapac‍cayā haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai dvai, adhipatiyā dvai, anantarai dvai, samanantarai dvai, sahajātai dvai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai dvai, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai dvai, kammai dvai, āhārai dvai, indriyai dvai, jhānai dvai, maggai dvai, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai dvai, atthiyā dvai, natthiyā dvai, vigatai dvai, avigatai dvai…pai॰…|

vipākadukaṃ

23. vipākapac‍cayā haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai aikaṃ, kammai nava, āhārai  nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ)|

anulaumagaṇanā|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

24. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu| (2)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (3)

25. vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

26. vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

27. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā  – vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu| (1)

28. vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

29. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

30. vipākañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (1)

31. vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

32. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ| yathā anulaumaṃ sahajātasadisaṃ|)

naanantarapac‍cayādi

33. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā … nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ, asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ (idaṃ nānattaṃ) naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

napuraijātapac‍cayau

34. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, paṭisandhikkhaṇai (saṃkhittaṃ)| (2)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| (3)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

35. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – arūpai naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai  vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

36. vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti napuraijātapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

37. vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati napuraijātapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

napacchājātapac‍cayādi

38. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati napacchājātapac‍cayā…pai॰… naāsaivanapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nakammapac‍cayau

39. vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai  paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammā caitanā|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammai khandhai paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā caitanā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ|

navipākapac‍cayau

40. vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti navipākapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

41. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca  rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ  mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naāhārapac‍cayau

42. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

naindriyapac‍cayau

43. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ|

najhānapac‍cayau

44. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

namaggapac‍cayau

45. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

vipākañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

nasampayuttapac‍cayau

46. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nasampayuttapac‍cayā… dvai|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nasampayuttapac‍cayā… dvai|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… aikaṃ|

navippayuttapac‍cayau

47. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vipākai aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā  – arūpai naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

naunatthi-nauvigatapac‍cayā

48. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

49. nahaituyā dasa, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā tairasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai  dvādasa, napacchājātai tairasa, naāsaivanai tairasa, nakammai dvai, navipākai pañ‍ca, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai dvai, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

nahaitudukaṃ

50. nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā dasa, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai tīṇi, napuraijātai dasa, napacchājātai dasa, naāsaivanai dasa, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai dvai, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi…pai॰… nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai aikaṃ, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai tīṇi, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai aikaṃ, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitumūlakaṃ|

(yathā  kusalattikai sajjhāyamaggaina gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ idhāpi gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

51. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā tairasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai tairasa, naāsaivanai tairasa, nakammai dvai, navipākai  pañ‍ca, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

tikaṃ

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, napuraijātai pañ‍ca, napacchājātai pañ‍ca, naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, navippayuttai tīṇi|

catuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰…|

aikādasakaṃ

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatipac‍cayā anantarapac‍cayā (mūlakaṃ saṃkhittaṃ) puraijātapac‍cayā napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā gaṇitā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

52. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai pañ‍ca, anantarai pañ‍ca, samanantarai pañ‍ca, sahajātai dasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai dasa, upanissayai pañ‍ca, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai dasa, vipākai nava, āhārai dasa, indriyai dasa, jhānai dasa, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai pañ‍ca, vippayuttai dasa, atthiyā dasa, natthiyā pañ‍ca, vigatai pañ‍ca, avigatai dasa|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi…pai॰…|

sattakaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā naadhipatipac‍cayā naanantarapac‍cayā nasamanantarapac‍cayā naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā sahajātai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, kammai tīṇi, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(yathā kusalattikai nahaitumūlakaṃ gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ| yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ vitthāritaṃ, aivaṃ idaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

53. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ sahajātau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

54. haituyā tairasa…pai॰… avigatai tairasa…pai॰…|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

55. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā ahaitukaṃ vipākaṃ  aikaṃ khandhaṃ sahajātā tayau khandhā, tayau  khandhai sahajātau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai sahajātā dvai khandhā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

56. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca…pai॰… navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰…|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

57. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai dasa…pai॰…|

sahajātavārau|

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

58. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ…pai॰…| (2)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ  pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ| (4)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

59. vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ| (2)

vipākañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ| (3)

60. vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

61. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

vipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhamma-dhammau  uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā, paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (3)

vipākañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, sauta… ghāna… jivhā… kāya… vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ  pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

62. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi (adhipatiyā paṭisandhikkhaṇai natthi)|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  pac‍cayā vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (4)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

63. vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vipākai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

64. vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti adhipatipac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vipākadhammadhammai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (3)

anantara-samanantarapac‍cayā

65. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā (ārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau
