66. sahajātapac‍cayā…pai॰… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākai khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu| (2)

vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā vatthu ca…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā vatthu ca| (3)

67. vipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai pac‍cayā dvai mahābhūtā; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākā khandhā| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (3)

vipākañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, sauta… ghāna… jivhā… kāya…pai॰… vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā  dvai khandhā| (1)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

nissayapac‍cayau

68. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nissayapac‍cayā (sahajātasadisaṃ)|

upanissaya-puraijātapac‍cayā

69. upanissayapac‍cayā… puraijātapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā, vatthuṃ puraijātapac‍cayā (anantarasadisaṃ| saṃkhittaṃ)|

āsaivanapac‍cayau

70. vipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… vatthuṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā| (2)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ  pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

71. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati kammapac‍cayā… tīṇi (sahajātasadisaṃ)|

vipākapac‍cayau

72. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā – aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai pac‍cayā dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ…pai॰… vipākau ca ubhayañ‍ca… tīṇi|

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca… tīṇi|

āhārapac‍cayādi

āhārapac‍cayā… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

73. haituyā sattarasa, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai sattarasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai sattarasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai sattarasa, vipākai nava, āhārai sattarasa, indriyai sattarasa, jhānai sattarasa, maggai sattarasa, sampayuttai  satta, vippayuttai sattarasa, atthiyā sattarasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai sattarasa|

haitudukaṃ

74. haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā sattarasa…pai॰… avigatai sattarasa|

(yathā kusalattikai gaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

75. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  pac‍cayā vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā vipākā khandhā, mahābhūtai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (4)

76. vipākañ‍ca  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā…pai॰… tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

77. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ| sabbāni padāni vitthāraitabbāni|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

78. nahaituyā dvādasa, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa, naanantarai pañ‍ca, nasamanantarai pañ‍ca, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai sattarasa, naāsaivanai sattarasa, nakammai cattāri, navipākai nava, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai cattāri, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

nahaitudukaṃ

79. nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā dvādasa, naanantarai tīṇi, nasamanantarai tīṇi…pai॰… nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai cattāri, naāhārai aikaṃ, naindriyai aikaṃ, najhānai cattāri, namaggai nava, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai dvai, naunatthiyā tīṇi, nauvigatai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

80. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā sattarasa…pai॰… nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

(yathā kusalattikai anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

81. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai satta, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai dvādasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai dvādasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai cattāri, kammai dvādasa, vipākai nava, āhārai dvādasa, indriyai dvādasa, jhānai dvādasa, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai dvādasa, atthiyā dvādasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai dvādasa|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇapac‍cayā sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyānulaumagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

pac‍cayavārau|

4. nissayavārau

1-4. pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

82. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ nissāya vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā…pai॰…|

haituyā  sattarasa…pai॰…|

nahaituyā dvādasa…pai॰… nauvigatai pañ‍ca|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca…pai॰… navippayuttai tīṇi|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai satta…pai॰… avigatai dvādasa|

nissayavārau|

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

83. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā| (saṃkhittaṃ| sabbāni padāni vitthāraitabbāni|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

84. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai tīṇi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai tīṇi, vipākai aikaṃ, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai tīṇi, jhānai tīṇi, maggai tīṇi, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā tīṇi, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai gaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

85. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (sabbāni padāni vibhajitabbāni|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

86. nahaituyā  tīṇi, na adhipatiyā tīṇi, napuraijātai tīṇi, napacchājātai tīṇi, naāsaivanai tīṇi, nakammai dvai, navipākai dvai, najhānai aikaṃ, namaggai dvai, navippayuttai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

87. haitupac‍cayā  naadhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… navippayuttai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikai anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

88. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… maggai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyānulaumagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

saṃsaṭṭhavārau|

6. sampayuttavārau

1-4. pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

89. vipākaṃ  dhammaṃ sampayuttau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ sampayuttā tayau khandhā…pai॰…|

haituyā  tīṇi…pai॰…|

nahaituyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

haitupac‍cayā naadhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰…|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰…|

sampayuttavārau|

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

90. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā haitū sappayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau – vipākā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ  rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā haitū kaṭattārūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau dhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

91. vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

92. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau  – vipākai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, kusalākusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| (1)

vipākau  dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saikkhā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vipākai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, caitaupariyañāṇaina vipākacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| vipākā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arahā phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, vipākai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina vipākacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| vipākā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

93. vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, saikkhā gautrabhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, saikkhā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, saikkhā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti, vipākadhammadhammai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, caitaupariyañāṇaina vipākadhammadhammacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanakusalaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanakusalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakusalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, kusalākusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanakusalaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanavipākassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanavipākassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arahā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati| arahā pahīnai  kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānāti, vipākadhammadhammai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, caitaupariyañāṇaina vipākadhammadhammacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanakusalaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanakiriyassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakiriyassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

94. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – arahā nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| nibbānaṃ āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| arahā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai … phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, caitaupariyañāṇaina naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānāti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanakiriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanakiriyassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanakiriyaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakiriyassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ phalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| kusalākusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammai  khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| kusalākusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saikkhā nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti; nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passanti, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ  suṇanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

adhipatipac‍cayau

95. vipākau  dhammau vipākassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – vipākādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākau dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – saikkhā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vipākai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

vipākau  dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – arahā phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sahajātādhipati – vipākādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākau dhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – vipākādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

96. vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, saikkhā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vipākadhammadhammai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati,| sahajātādhipati – vipākadhammadhammādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – arahā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sahajātādhipati – vipākadhammadhammādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – vipākadhammadhammādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – arahā nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati| sahajātādhipati – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  vipākassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – saikkhā nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, maggassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

97. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vipākā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipākamanaudhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākamanaudhātu vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bhavaṅgaṃ āvaj‍janāya anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu kiriyamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa … anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau  vipākassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā  vuṭṭhānassa… maggau phalassa… saikkhānaṃ anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakusalaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā pañ‍can‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa, arahatau anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanakiriyaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā vipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

samanantarapac‍cayau

98. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (anantarasadisaṃ)|

sahajātapac‍cayau

99. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aikau khandhau…pai॰… tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau aikau khandhau…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  vipākassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

100. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aikau khandhau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa  añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā khandhā vatthussa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau dhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vatthussa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (3)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  vipākassa dhammassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (2)

vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākassa dhammassa…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca…pai॰…| (1) (satta pañhā)|

nissayapac‍cayau

101. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa  nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa, vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu vipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākassa dhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasahagatau aikau khandhau ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagatau aikau khandhau ca kāyāyatanañ‍ca…pai॰… vipākau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca…pai॰…| (1)

vipākau  ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa…pai॰… vipākā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ) paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākadhammadhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca…pai॰…| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ…| (2) (tairasa pañhā)|

upanissayapac‍cayau

102. vipākau  dhammau vipākassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phalasamāpatti kāyikassa sukhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākau dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau  dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – arahā kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kiriyasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍jati, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kiriyasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍jati…pai॰…| (3)

103. vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… mānaṃ jappaiti… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti| rāgaṃ… dausaṃ… mauhaṃ… mānaṃ… diṭṭhiṃ… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti, pāṇaṃ  hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati…pai॰… saddhā…pai॰… patthanā saddhāya… sīlassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa parikammaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰… paṭhamassa maggassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa…pai॰… catutthassa maggassa parikammaṃ catutthassa…pai॰… paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saikkhā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kusalasamāpattiṃ uppādainti…pai॰… maggau saikkhānaṃ atthappaṭisambhidāya…pai॰… ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pāṇātipātau pāṇātipātassa…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā…pai॰… byāpādassa…pai॰… mātughātikammaṃ mātughātikammassa…pai॰… niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā…pai॰… niyatamicchādiṭṭhi niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā…pai॰… saṅghabhaidakakammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau  – saddhaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, pariyiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhauti…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti  …pai॰… saddhā…pai॰… patthanā kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalākusalaṃ kammaṃ vipākassa kammavipākassa (syā॰) upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – arahā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kiriyasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ samāpaj‍jati, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| maggau arahatau atthappaṭisambhidāya…pai॰… ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

104. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau  – arahā utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ kiriyasamāpattiṃ uppādaiti…pai॰…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau – utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ, kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa, phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau  …pai॰…|

pakatūpanissayau  – utuṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| utu… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

puraijātapac‍cayau

105. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ|

ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – arahā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ|

ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, assādainti abhinandanti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| kusalākusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijāta…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰…| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ|

ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ…pai॰… ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ  uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ…pai॰… dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ…pai॰…|

vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu vipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

106. vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā vipākā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

107. vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā vipākadhammadhammā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ vipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā…pai॰… pac‍cayau| (1)

kammapac‍cayau

108. vipākau  dhammau vipākassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā caitanā…pai॰…| (1)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā caitanā kaṭattārūpānaṃ…pai॰… caitanā vatthussa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau  dhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – vipākadhammadhammā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – vipākadhammadhammā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – vipākadhammadhammā caitanā kaṭattārūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – vipākadhammadhammā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

vipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākadhammadhammā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

109. vipākau  dhammau vipākassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai  vipākā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa…pai॰…| (2)

vipākau dhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

āhārapac‍cayau

110. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ… tīṇi| (paṭisandhipi imaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ kātabbā|)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa  āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

indriyapac‍cayau

111. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi (paṭisandhi kātabbā kātabbaṃ (sī॰)|) vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; kāyindriyaṃ…pai॰…| (2)

vipākau  ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – cakkhundriyañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… kāyindriyañ‍ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagatānaṃ khandhānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

jhānapac‍cayau

112. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau … tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā jhānaṅgā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

maggapac‍cayau

113. vipākau dhammau vipākassa dhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā maggaṅgā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

sampayuttapac‍cayau

114. vipākau  dhammau vipākassa dhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

115. vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – vipākā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā  khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ  vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; khandhā vatthussa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ  – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu vipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

atthipac‍cayau

116. vipākau  dhammau vipākassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ…pai॰…| (1)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa…pai॰… sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – vipākā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikhaṇai vipākā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau  dhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (3)

117. vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca…pai॰… vipākadhammadhammau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (3)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa  …pai॰… sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ upādārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

puraijātaṃ – arahā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti, vatthu naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pacchājātā – naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātaṃ – paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… assādainti; taṃ ārabbha rāgau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ…pai॰… kusalākusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā…pai॰… sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ…pai॰… vipākau tadārammaṇatā…pai॰… rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ  kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā cakkhuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… assādainti…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… vatthuṃ anic‍catau…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| dibbaina cakkhunā…pai॰… vatthu vipākadhammadhammānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

118. vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasahagatau aikau khandhau ca cakkhāyatanañ‍ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasahagatau…pai॰… vipākau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ…pai॰…| (1)

vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – vipākā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai vipākā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

pacchājātā – vipākā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa…pai॰… pacchājātā vipākā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau  ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākadhammadhammassa…pai॰… sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – vipākadhammadhammau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ…pai॰…| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau  ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – vipākadhammadhammā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthi-vigatāvigatapac‍cayā

119. vipākau dhammau…| (natthi-vigataṃ anantarasadisaṃ, avigataṃ atthisadisaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

120. haituyā satta, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai aikādasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai nava, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai satta, indriyai nava, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa|

sabhāgaṃ

haitupac‍cayā adhipatiyā satta, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍ca, nissayai satta, vipākai tīṇi, indriyai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, avigatai satta|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumagaṇanā gaṇitā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbā|)

anulaumaṃ

pac‍canīyuddhārau

121. vipākau  dhammau vipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākau dhammau vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākau dhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākau dhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākadhammadhammau vipākassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|  (4)

vipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau vipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau  vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ… puraijātaṃ| (1)

vipākau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa sahajātaṃ… pacchājātaṃ… āhāraṃ… indriyaṃ| (2)

vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā vipākadhammadhammassa sahajātaṃ… puraijātaṃ| (1)

vipākadhammadhammau ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa sahajātaṃ… pacchājātaṃ… āhāraṃ… indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

122. nahaituyā  sauḷasa, naārammaṇai sauḷasa, naadhipatiyā sauḷasa, naanantarai sauḷasa, nasamanantarai sauḷasa, nasahajātai dvādasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvādasa, nanissayai dvādasa, naupanissayai sauḷasa, napuraijātai cuddasa, napacchājātai sauḷasa, naāsaivanai sauḷasa, nakammai pan‍narasa, navipākai cuddasa, naāhārai sauḷasa, naindriyai sauḷasa, najhānai sauḷasa, namaggai sauḷasa, nasampayuttai dvādasa, navippayuttai dasa, nauatthiyā dasa, naunatthiyā sauḷasa, nauvigatai sauḷasa, nauavigatai dasa|

sabhāgaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai sauḷasa…pai॰… nauavigatai dasa|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyagaṇanā vitthāritā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitusabhāgaṃ

123. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai satta, navipākai cattāri, naāhārai satta, naindriyai satta, najhānai satta, namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta|

ghaṭanā

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti  naārammaṇai satta…pai॰… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi…pai॰… navipākai cattāri…pai॰… nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai satta|

(yathā kusalattikai anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā  vitthāritā aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ| asammauhantaina aisau sajjhāyamaggau|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

124. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā dasa, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai aikādasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai nava, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai nava, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai satta, indriyai nava, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatiyā satta…pai॰… avigatai tairasa|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyānulaumagaṇanā vitthāritā, aivaṃ vitthāratabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

vipākattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. upādin‍nattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ khandhā; upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ  khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

2. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…|

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

adhipatipac‍cayau

3. anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anantarapac‍cayādi

4. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā… samanantarapac‍cayā… sahajātapac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca  kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ|

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati sahajātapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ  …pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi|

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… anupādin‍nupādāniyau…pai॰…| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau…pai॰…| (1)

añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayau

5. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā vatthu ca…pai॰… khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā; asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

nissaya-upanissayapac‍cayā

6. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nissayapac‍cayā… nava| upanissayapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

puraijātapac‍cayau

āsaivanapac‍cayau

8. anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati āsaivanapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

vipākapac‍cayau

10. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati vipākapac‍cayā… tīṇi|
