āhārapac‍cayādi

11. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati āhārapac‍cayā…pai॰… indriyapac‍cayā… jhānapac‍cayā… maggapac‍cayā… sampayuttapac‍cayā… vippayuttapac‍cayā… atthipac‍cayā… natthipac‍cayā… vigatapac‍cayā… avigatapac‍cayā|

(yathā kusalattikassa paṭic‍cavārau sajjhāyamaggaina vitthāritau, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

12. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai dvai, kammai nava, vipākai nava, āhārai nava, indriyai nava, jhānai nava, maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava, atthiyā nava, natthiyā tīṇi, vigatai tīṇi, avigatai nava|

haitudukaṃ

haitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai nava|

(yathā kusalattikai paṭic‍cagaṇanā sajjhāyamaggaina gaṇitā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

13. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā, ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai  paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca upādārūpaṃ| vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca  anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

14. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – paṭisandhikkhaṇai upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ, khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca …pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca  anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naadhipatipac‍cayau

15. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā (paṭisandhi paripuṇṇaṃ)… tīṇi|

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… aikā|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyā adhipati| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ|

naanantarapac‍cayādi

16. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā…pai॰… nasamanantarapac‍cayā…. naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā… napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāsaivanapac‍cayā – vipākaṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nakammapac‍cayau

17. anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā  – anupādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyā caitanā| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyā caitanā| (2)

navipākapac‍cayau

18. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā… aikā|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalaṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā… tīṇi|

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – kusalai anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

naāhārapac‍cayau

19. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

naindriyapac‍cayau

20. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naindriyapac‍cayā – asañ‍ñasattānaṃ mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca rūpajīvitindriyaṃ| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naindriyapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

najhānapac‍cayau

21. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasahagataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca  …pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati najhānapac‍cayā – bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

namaggapac‍cayau

22. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati namaggapac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā… pañ‍ca|

nasampayuttapac‍cayau

23. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nasampayuttapac‍cayā (naārammaṇapac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

navippayuttapac‍cayau

24. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayaukhandhā…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navippayuttapac‍cayā – arūpai anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

naunatthi-nauvigatapac‍cayā

25. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā|

(yathā kusalattikai  pac‍canīyavārau vitthāritau, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbau|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

26. nahaituyā  pañ‍ca, naārammaṇai cha, naadhipatiyā cha, naanantarai cha, nasamanantarai cha, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, naupanissayai cha, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai dvai, navipākai cha, naāhārai dvai, naindriyai dvai, najhānai dvai, namaggai pañ‍ca, nasampayuttai cha, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā cha, nauvigatai cha|

nahaitudukaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri, naadhipatiyā pañ‍ca, naanantarai cattāri…pai॰… naāsaivanai pañ‍ca, nakammai aikaṃ, navipākai dvai…pai॰… najhānai dvai, namaggai pañ‍ca, nasampayuttai cattāri, navippayuttai dvai…pai॰… nauvigatai cattāri|

(yathā kusalattikai gaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

27. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cha…pai॰… napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai dvai, navipākai cha, nasampayuttai cha, navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai cha|

(yathā kusalattikai anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbā|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

28. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai dvai…pai॰… sahajātai pañ‍ca, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai dvai, nissayai pañ‍ca, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai dvai, āsaivanai aikaṃ, kammai pañ‍ca…pai॰… jhānai pañ‍ca, maggai aikaṃ, sampayuttai dvai, vippayuttai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyānulaumagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbā|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

paṭic‍cavārau|

2. sahajātavārau

1-4. pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

29. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ sahajātau upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (paṭic‍cavāraupi sahajātavāraupi sadisau|)

sahajātavārau|

3. pac‍cayavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

30. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… khandhai pac‍cayā vatthu, vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai pac‍cayā kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (2)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā khandhā| (3)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (4)

31. anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… aikā|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca  pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

32. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā khandhā; cakkhāyatanaṃ  pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (2)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā khandhā| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

adhipatipac‍cayau

33. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā khandhā| (2)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā… aikā|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tissau|

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca  anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… adhipatipac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… adhipatipac‍cayā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati adhipatipac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

anantarapac‍cayau

34. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati anantarapac‍cayā|

(catuvīsatipac‍cayā vitthāraitabbā, avigatapac‍cayā|)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

35. haituyā aikādasa, ārammaṇai satta, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai aikādasa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai satta, nissayai aikādasa, upanissayai satta, puraijātai satta, āsaivanai cha, kammai aikādasa, vipākai aikādasa, āhārai aikādasa, indriyai aikādasa, jhānai aikādasa, maggai aikādasa, sampayuttai satta, vippayuttai aikādasa, atthiyā aikādasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai aikādasa|

haitudukaṃ

haitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai satta…pai॰… avigatai aikādasa|

(yathā kusalattikai gaṇanā aivaṃ gaṇaitabbā|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

nahaitupac‍cayau

36. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… cakkhāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ pac‍cayā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā upādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā ahaitukā anupādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (2)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… rūpaṃ; vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukai upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  rūpaṃ; ahaitukaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā; vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

37. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

naadhipatipac‍cayau

38. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (2)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā adhipati| (3)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naadhipatipac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ pac‍cayā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰…| (4)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā| (aikā pañhā|)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā adhipati| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā  anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati na adhipatipac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā adhipati| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naadhipatipac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca pac‍cayā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ; anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā| (1)

naanantarapac‍cayādi

39. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naanantarapac‍cayā… nasamanantarapac‍cayā… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayā… naupanissayapac‍cayā… napuraijātapac‍cayā… napacchājātapac‍cayā… naāsaivanapac‍cayā…pai॰…|

nakammapac‍cayau

40. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā caitanā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā caitanā| (2)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā caitanā| bāhiraṃ… āhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ… utusamuṭṭhānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā caitanā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – kusalai anupādin‍naanupādāniyai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā kusalā caitanā| (1)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nakammapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā caitanā| (1)

navipākapac‍cayau

41. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ pac‍cayā…pai॰… (1)|

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā; vatthuṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyā khandhā| (2)

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – vatthuṃ pac‍cayā kusalā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā khandhā| (3)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… aikā|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā  anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati navipākapac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai ca vatthuñ‍ca pac‍cayā dvai khandhā|

naāhārapac‍cayādi

42. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati naāhārapac‍cayā… naindriyapac‍cayā… najhānapac‍cayā… namaggapac‍cayā… nasampayuttapac‍cayā… navippayuttapac‍cayā… naunatthipac‍cayā… nauvigatapac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ)|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

43. nahaituyā pañ‍ca, naārammaṇai cha, naadhipatiyā aṭṭha, naanantarai cha, nasamanantarai cha, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai cha, naupanissayai cha, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai aikādasa, naāsaivanai aikādasa, nakammai cha, navipākai dasa, naāhārai dvai, naindriyai dvai, najhānai dvai, namaggai pañ‍ca, na sampayuttai cha, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā cha, nauvigatai cha (vitthāraina gaṇaitabbaṃ)|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

44. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cha…pai॰… nauvigatai cha| (vitthāraina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

pac‍cayavārau|

4. nissayavārau

46. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ nissāya upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ nissāya tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai nissāya… (saṃkhittaṃ)| (pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi sadisau)|

nissayavārau|

5. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

haitupac‍cayau

47. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau aikau khandhau, dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā, paṭisandhikkhaṇai upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

saṅkhyāvārau

48. haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… āsaivanai dvai…pai॰… vipākai dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

(yathā  kusalattikai gaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbā|)

anulaumaṃ|

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

49. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| (1)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhā tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhā dvai khandhā; vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai saṃsaṭṭhau vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituyā dvai, na adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… navippayuttai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)
