5. saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

1. paṭic‍cavārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

1. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ; khandhai paṭic‍ca vatthu, vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca khandhā; aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| (1)

2. asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ (saṃkhittaṃ)| (1)

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

3. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā nava, anantarai tīṇi, samanantarai tīṇi, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai nava, upanissayai tīṇi, puraijātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai nava, vipākai pañ‍ca, āhārai nava…pai॰… maggai nava, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai vibhattaṃ, aivaṃ vibhajitabbaṃ|)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

4. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagatai uddhac‍casahagatai khandhai paṭic‍ca vicikicchāsahagatau uddhac‍casahagatau mauhau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā – ahaitukaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… ahaitukapaṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|) (1)

(yathā kusalattikai vibhattaṃ, aivaṃ vibhajitabbaṃ|)

suddhaṃ

5. nahaituyā  dvai, naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā cha, naanantarai pañ‍ca…pai॰… naupanissayai pañ‍ca, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai  nava, naāhārai aikaṃ…pai॰… namaggai aikaṃ, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

6. haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai pañ‍ca, naadhipatiyā cha…pai॰… napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai nava, naāsaivanai nava, nakammai tīṇi, navipākai nava, nasampayuttai pañ‍ca, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā pañ‍ca, nauvigatai pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

7. nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai dvai…pai॰… vipākai aikaṃ…pai॰… maggai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

paṭic‍cavārau|

2-6 sahajāta-pac‍caya-nissaya-saṃsaṭṭha-sampayuttavārau

(sahajātavāraupi pac‍cayavāraupi nissayavāraupi saṃsaṭṭhavāraupi sampayuttavāraupi vitthāraitabbau|)

7. pañhāvārau

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

1. vibhaṅgavārau

haitupac‍cayau

8. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā haitū cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā haitū sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

9. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – rāgaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uddhac‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| diṭṭhiṃ assādaiti…pai॰… vicikicchaṃ ārabbha…pai॰… uddhac‍caṃ ārabbha…pai॰… daumanassaṃ ārabbha…pai॰…| (1)

(yathā kusalattikai vibhattaṃ, aivaṃ vibhajitabbaṃ|)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā pahīnai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, vikkhambhitai kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhanti, pubbai samudāciṇṇai kilaisai jānanti, saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti; saikkhā vā puthuj‍janā vā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti, kusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati; saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai assādainti abhinandanti…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, akusalai niruddhai vipākau tadārammaṇatā uppaj‍jati| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

10. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni pac‍cavaikkhati; jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhati, ariyā gautrabhuṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti; sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti; dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passanti, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇanti, caitaupariyañāṇaina asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā iddhividhañāṇassa, caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, yathākammūpagañāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; pubbai suciṇṇāni assādaiti…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ assādaiti…pai॰… cakkhuṃ assādaiti| phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ… asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – nibbānaṃ maggassa, phalassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanti| nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ariyā caitaupariyañāṇaina asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikacittasamaṅgissa cittaṃ jānanti| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā caitaupariyañāṇassa, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇassa, anāgataṃsañāṇassa, āvaj‍janāya ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

adhipatipac‍cayau

11. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – rāgaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati; diṭṭhiṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sahajātādhipati – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

12. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, pubbai suciṇṇāni garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhati, saikkhā gautrabhuṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, vaudānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti| sahajātādhipati – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇādhipati – dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā taṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati, pubbai suciṇṇāni  garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati…pai॰… jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā jhānaṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati…pai॰… cakkhuṃ garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai…pai॰… vatthuṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikai khandhai garuṃ katvā assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ garuṃ katvā rāgau uppaj‍jati, diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – nibbānaṃ maggassa, phalassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇādhipati, sahajātādhipati| ārammaṇādhipati – ariyā maggā vuṭṭhahitvā maggaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, phalaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ garuṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhanti, nibbānaṃ gautrabhussa, vaudānassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikādhipati cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahajātādhipati – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikādhipati sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

anantarapac‍cayau

13. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa… āvaj‍janā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – āvaj‍janā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa … anulaumaṃ phalasamāpattiyā… niraudhā vuṭṭhahantassa naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ phalasamāpattiyā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| maggau phalassa… phalaṃ phalassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – phalaṃ vuṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

samanantarapac‍cayādi

14. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau  …pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati…pai॰… dausaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati| rāgau…pai॰… patthanā rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pāṇātipātau pāṇātipātassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… niyatamicchādiṭṭhi niyatamicchādiṭṭhiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ… vipassanaṃ… abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| rāgau…pai॰… patthanā saddhāya…pai॰… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pāṇaṃ hantvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ  uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaiti…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhinditvā tassa paṭighātatthāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti| akusalaṃ kammaṃ vipākassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pakatūpanissayau – rāgaṃ upanissāya maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jati| dausaṃ…pai॰… patthanaṃ upanissāya maggaṃ uppādaiti, phalasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jati| rāgau…pai॰… patthanā maggassa… phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

15. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| sīlaṃ… sutaṃ… cāgaṃ… pañ‍ñaṃ… kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ… kāyikaṃ  dukkhaṃ… utuṃ… bhaujanaṃ… saināsanaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… samāpattiṃ uppādaiti| saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ saddhāya…pai॰… kāyikassa sukhassa, kāyikassa dukkhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalaṃ kammaṃ vipākassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭhamassa jhānassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa jhānassa…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa…pai॰…| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – saddhaṃ upanissāya mānaṃ jappaiti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; sīlaṃ…pai॰… saināsanaṃ upanissāya pāṇaṃ hanati…pai॰… saṅghaṃ bhindati, saddhā…pai॰… saināsanaṃ rāgassa…pai॰… patthanāya upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamassa maggassa parikammaṃ paṭhamassa maggassa…pai॰… dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyassa maggassa…pai॰… catutthassa maggassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

16. asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – paṭhamau maggau dutiyassa maggassa…pai॰… dutiyau maggau tatiyassa maggassa…pai॰… tatiyau maggau catutthassa  maggassa…pai॰… maggau phalasamāpattiyā upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇūpanissayau, anantarūpanissayau, pakatūpanissayau…pai॰…| pakatūpanissayau – ariyā maggaṃ upanissāya anuppan‍naṃ samāpattiṃ uppādainti, uppan‍naṃ samāpattiṃ samāpaj‍janti, saṅkhārai anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassanti| maggau ariyānaṃ atthappaṭisambhidāya…pai॰… ṭhānāṭhānakausal‍lassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| phalasamāpatti kāyikassa sukhassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

puraijātapac‍cayau

17. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati; sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… rūpai… saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthupuraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ, vatthupuraijātaṃ| ārammaṇapuraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati| vatthupuraijātaṃ – vatthu saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vatthu asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

pacchājātapac‍cayau

18. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – pacchājātā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| (1)

āsaivanapac‍cayau

19. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – purimā purimā…pai॰… anulaumaṃ gautrabhussa… anulaumaṃ vaudānassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – gautrabhu maggassa… vaudānaṃ maggassa āsaivanapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

kammapac‍cayau

20. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca  dhammassa  kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰…| nānākkhaṇikā – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā caitanā vipākānaṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā, nānākkhaṇikā| sahajātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nānākkhaṇikā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā kusalā caitanā vipākānaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā caitanā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātā  asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā caitanā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

vipākapac‍cayau

21. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kaṭattā ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau =19 …pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰… khandhā vatthussa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā dvin‍naṃ khandhānaṃ…pai॰…| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2) asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – vipākau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ vipākapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (3)

āhārapac‍cayau

22. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā āhārā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

indriyapac‍cayau

23. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā indriyā sampayuttakānaṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau… tīṇi|

jhānapac‍cayādi

24. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa jhānapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… maggapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sampayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

vippayuttapac‍cayau

25. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau; paṭisandhikkhaṇai asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā kaṭattārūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| khandhā vatthussa…pai॰… vatthu khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikānaṃ  khandhānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa vippayuttapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

atthipac‍cayau

26. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā  – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (3)

27. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātau – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… asañ‍ñasattānaṃ aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati| sautaṃ…pai॰… kāyaṃ… rūpai…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbai… vatthuṃ anic‍catau dukkhatau anattatau vipassati, dibbaina cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, dibbāya sautadhātuyā saddaṃ suṇāti| rūpāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cakkhāyatanaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa…pai॰… vatthu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kabaḷīkārau āhārau imassa kāyassa…pai॰… rūpajīvitindriyaṃ kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – cakkhuṃ assādaiti abhinandati; taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jati…pai॰… daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati; vatthuṃ assādaiti…pai॰… vatthu saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikānaṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| puraijātaṃ – vatthu asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikānaṃ  khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

28. asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṅkiliṭṭhasaṃkilisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ| sahajātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (3)

29. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ  atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| sahajātau – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau aikau khandhau ca vatthu ca tiṇṇan‍naṃ khandhānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… dvai khandhā…pai॰…| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau – sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| sahajātā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā ca mahābhūtā ca cittasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau ca puraijātassa imassa kāyassa atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pacchājātā  – saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā khandhā ca rūpajīvitindriyañ‍ca kaṭattārūpānaṃ atthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

natthi-vigatāvigatapac‍cayā

30. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa natthipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… vigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… avigatapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

2. saṅkhyāvārau

suddhaṃ

31. haituyā  satta, ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā aṭṭha, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai aṭṭha, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai cattāri, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa|

haitusabhāgaṃ

haitupac‍cayā adhipatiyā cattāri, sahajātai satta, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai satta, vipākai cattāri, indriyai cattāri, maggai cattāri, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai tīṇi, atthiyā satta, avigatai satta|

haitusāmañ‍ñaghaṭanā (9)

32. haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti  satta| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissayaatthi-avigatanti tīṇi| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-sampayutta-atthi-avigatanti  tīṇi| haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti tīṇi| (avipākaṃ – 4)

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti cattāri| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ñanissaya-vipāka-atthi-avigatanti dvai| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipākasampayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai| haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-vipāka-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti dvai| haitu-sahajāta-añ‍ñamañ‍ña-nissaya-vipāka-vippayutta-atthi-avigatanti aikaṃ| (savipākaṃ – 5) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

anulaumaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

33. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (4)

34. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… indriyapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa  upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa ca dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (1)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (2)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, puraijātaṃ| (1)

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikassa dhammassa sahajātaṃ, pacchājātaṃ, āhāraṃ, indriyaṃ| (2)

2. pac‍cayapac‍canīyaṃ

suddhaṃ

35. nahaituyā  cuddasa, naārammaṇai cuddasa, naadhipatiyā cuddasa, naanantarai cuddasa, nasamanantarai cuddasa, nasahajātai dasa, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai dasa, nanissayai dasa, naupanissayai tairasa, napuraijātai dvādasa, napacchājātai cuddasa, naāsaivanai cuddasa, nakammai cuddasa, navipākai cuddasa, naāhārai cuddasa, naindriyai cuddasa, najhānai cuddasa, namaggai cuddasa, nasampayuttai dasa, navippayuttai aṭṭha, nauatthiyā aṭṭha, naunatthiyā cuddasa, nauvigatai cuddasa, nauavigatai aṭṭha|

dukaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cuddasa (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyagaṇanā, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyaṃ|

3. pac‍cayānulaumapac‍canīyaṃ

haitudukaṃ

36. haitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta, nasamanantarai satta, naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, naupanissayai satta, napuraijātai satta, napacchājātai satta, naāsaivanai satta, nakammai satta, navipākai satta, naāhārai satta, naindriyai satta, najhānai satta, namaggai satta, nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi, naunatthiyā satta, nauvigatai satta|

ghaṭanā

haitu-sahajāta-nissaya-atthi-avigatanti naārammaṇai satta…pai॰… naañ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi…pai॰… nasampayuttai tīṇi, navippayuttai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai satta (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai anulaumapac‍canīyagaṇanā vibhattā, aivaṃ vibhajitabbaṃ|)

anulaumapac‍canīyaṃ|

4. pac‍cayapac‍canīyānulaumaṃ

nahaitudukaṃ

37. nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā aṭṭha, anantarai satta, samanantarai satta, sahajātai nava, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai tīṇi, nissayai tairasa, upanissayai aṭṭha, puraijātai tīṇi, pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai tīṇi, kammai satta, vipākai cattāri, āhārai satta, indriyai satta, jhānai satta, maggai satta, sampayuttai tīṇi, vippayuttai pañ‍ca, atthiyā tairasa, natthiyā satta, vigatai satta, avigatai tairasa|

tikaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayā  naārammaṇapac‍cayā adhipatiyā satta (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai pac‍canīyānulaumagaṇanā vibhattā, aivaṃ vibhajitabbaṃ|)

pac‍canīyānulaumaṃ|

saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭṭhānapāḷi paṭhamau bhāgau niṭṭhitau|
